<h2 class="pdd-q">What is the answer for your seat?</h2>
<p class="pdd-a"><b class="pdd-s1-lead">Rules catch, AI flags, a rule or a person releases.</b> Three questions, each with a verdict, one number and the screen that proves it.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: savings, losses and the first check.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: where agents assist, and what to build first.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">For you: who earns, and which rules to watch.</span></p>

Of a 12-step process.">&gt;80%</span></span><span class="pdd-s1-cap">of W-9 steps cut, BILL says</span></p><p class="pdd-s1-why">Tax forms and invoice coding run in production on <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="H#12 H#10" data-note="BILL reports both figures.">vendor figures</span>.</span> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="H#30" data-note="As reported at Sibos 2026, through a newsletter citing a vendor story.">No bank found</span> lets a model release money unsupervised.</p><a class="pdd-pill pdd-s1-go" href="#pdd-ai">Where AI fits<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6"/></svg></a></article>

Reported losses only, so a floor, not a rate. Latest B2B data, Q2 2026: B2B ACH up 9.9% to 2.2 billion payments.">about 0.003% of US ACH value</span>. Small for the system, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="L#1" data-note="Our arithmetic: $3.05B divided by 24,768 complaints.">about $123,000</span> per complaint.</p><p class="pdd-s1-why">#2 FBI loss type. #1 is investment fraud, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="L#1" data-note="FBI IC3 2025: investment fraud losses.">$8.65B</span>, with crypto investment scams alone at <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="L#1" data-note="FBI IC3 2025: crypto investment scam losses.">$7.2B</span>.</p><a class="pdd-pill pdd-s1-go" href="#pdd-path">Money path<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6"/></svg></a></article>

<section class="pdd-scene pdd-s4-scene" id="pdd-ai" aria-labelledby="pdd-s4-q">
<p class="pdd-kicker"><b>2</b>AI agents</p>
<h2 class="pdd-q" id="pdd-s4-q">Where does AI earn a place?</h2>
<p class="pdd-a">Four of nine use cases run in production with vendor numbers, and two remain unproven.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: which AI saves money today, and the guard.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: what each agent does, and who releases.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">For you: where agent volume and pricing stand.</span></p>
<div class="pdd-stage">
<div class="pdd-ag" id="pdd-agents" aria-label="Top three agentic payment use cases">
<p class="pdd-label pdd-ag-top">Top three agentic use cases</p>
<div class="pdd-ag-row">
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-ag-tile" data-m="flow" data-rise><div class="pdd-ag-head"><svg class="pdd-ag-icon" viewBox="0 0 80 80" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><rect x="10" y="22" width="26" height="36" rx="4"/><path d="M16 32h14M16 42h10"/><rect x="48" y="12" width="22" height="20" rx="6"/><path d="M59 12V6"/><circle cx="55" cy="22" r="2"/><circle cx="63" cy="22" r="2"/><path d="M40 44h22M56 38l6 6-6 6"/><path d="M48 62h22"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-ag-name">B2B supplier agents</h3></div><p class="pdd-ag-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-ag-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#2" data-note="Melio: over $100M annualised agent payment volume, July 2026. A vendor figure.">&gt;$100M</span></span><span class="pdd-ag-cap">a year at one network</span><span class="pdd-tag">Vendor figure</span></p><p class="pdd-ag-now"><b>Today</b> Melio says live since <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#2" data-note="Melio launched its agent payment network on June 17, 2026. Melio&#39;s own claim.">June 2026</span> <span class="pdd-tag">Vendor claim</span>. A Visa, BILL and Billtrust pilot began <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#8 N#9" data-note="Visa calls it the first live pilot through supplier portals. Visa&#39;s own claim. No liability framework is set.">October 20, 2026</span>.</p><dl class="pdd-ag-out"><dt>Problem</dt><dd>An agent can pay the wrong supplier.</dd><dt>Root cause</dt><dd>Only <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#3" data-note="Deloitte research, March 2026, as reported by MarketScale. Suppliers whose front and back office are completely integrated.">13%</span> of suppliers are fully integrated.</dd><dt>6 months</dt><dd><span class="pdd-view">More payables tools ship it. A person approves payee changes.</span></dd><dt>12 months</dt><dd><span class="pdd-view">Banks add agent rules to payment terms.</span></dd></dl></article>
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-ag-tile" data-m="coin" data-rise><div class="pdd-ag-head"><svg class="pdd-ag-icon" viewBox="0 0 80 80" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M10 16h8l7 32h32l7-22H22"/><circle cx="30" cy="60" r="4"/><circle cx="54" cy="60" r="4"/><rect x="44" y="4" width="22" height="18" rx="5"/><circle cx="51" cy="13" r="2"/><circle cx="59" cy="13" r="2"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-ag-name">Consumer agent checkout</h3></div><p class="pdd-ag-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-ag-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#4" data-note="Global merchant chargeback costs in 2026, a Mastercard forecast cited by Chargeflow. No agent checkout market is sized yet.">$36.9B</span></span><span class="pdd-ag-cap">chargebacks agent rules must cover</span><span class="pdd-tag">Vendor forecast</span></p><p class="pdd-ag-now"><b>Today</b> Live at <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#8 M#7" data-note="Shopify opened Shop MCP on October 13, 2026. Google began a US rollout on October 15, 2026.">Shopify and Google</span>.</span> ChatGPT ended Instant Checkout in <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#17" data-note="ForkLog, March 2026. Shoppers now finish on retailer sites.">March 2026</span>.</span> Buyers still confirm.</p><dl class="pdd-ag-out"><dt>Problem</dt><dd>An agent buys the wrong thing, or too much.</dd><dt>Root cause</dt><dd>Card <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#4" data-note="Chargeflow, July 2026, a vendor blog. Reason codes describe the cardholder&#39;s claim, not the agent&#39;s role.">dispute rules</span> were written for human buyers.</dd><dt>6 months</dt><dd><span class="pdd-view">More volume.</span></dd><dt>12 months</dt><dd><span class="pdd-view">Card disputes weigh agent signals.</span></dd></dl></article>
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-ag-tile" data-m="rule" data-rise><div class="pdd-ag-head"><svg class="pdd-ag-icon" viewBox="0 0 80 80" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><rect x="8" y="20" width="26" height="40" rx="5"/><path d="M14 32h14M14 42h8"/><rect x="46" y="20" width="26" height="40" rx="5"/><path d="M52 32h14"/><path d="M34 34h12M40 46h-6M40 46h6"/><path d="M52 46h14M52 52h10"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-ag-name">Agent-to-API (x402)</h3></div><p class="pdd-ag-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-ag-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#5" data-note="Crypto Briefing, 30 days to mid August 2026. Trackers disagree on totals.">$24M</span></span><span class="pdd-ag-cap">in 30 days</span><span class="pdd-tag">Tracker estimate</span></p><p class="pdd-ag-now"><b>Today</b> A Linux Foundation project with <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#6" data-note="x402 Foundation, October 1, 2026.">50+ members</span>. Volume is <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#5" data-note="Crypto Briefing reports activity fell from its 2025 highs.">below its peak</span>.</p><dl class="pdd-ag-out"><dt>Problem</dt><dd>Agents pay per API call, with no account.</dd><dt>Root cause</dt><dd>Tickets average about <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#27" data-note="Our arithmetic: $24.24M over 75.41M transactions in 30 days. x402.org, August 2026.">32 cents</span>,</span> <span class="pdd-view">too small for card fees.</span></dd><dt>6 months</dt><dd><span class="pdd-view">Tens of millions a month or less.</span></dd><dt>12 months</dt><dd><span class="pdd-view">Grows only if AP2 adds new rails.</span></dd></dl></article>
</div>
<div class="pdd-ag-foot"><details class="pdd-more pdd-ag-who"><summary>Who pays when an agent pays the wrong supplier?</summary><ul class="pdd-s5-al"><li>Under <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#1" data-note="Goodwin, June 2026. A law firm reading of UCC Article 4A.">UCC 4A</span>, <span class="pdd-view">a business payment generally binds the payer if the bank followed the agreed security procedure.</span></li><li>Melio says its <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#2" data-note="A Melio claim, launched June 17, 2026. A vendor figure.">agent payment network</span> is live for small businesses.</li><li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#3" data-note="Deloitte research, March 2026, as reported by MarketScale.">38%</span> of B2B buyers already use agentic AI in purchasing.</li><li>The same payee change guard applies. An agent should never edit bank details or release money. <a href="#pdd-guard">Payee change guard</a></li></ul></details></div>
</div>
<p class="pdd-s4-sr">Nine AI use cases sit on the payout flow. Four are in production with vendor numbers: invoice coding, tax forms, sanctions alerts, exceptions and returns. Two are in production with no numbers: fraud detection and reconciliation. Agent payments are a pilot, though one vendor says live. Bank detail change and rail choice are unproven. Our illustration for the United States gives $4.5 billion a year in the default case and $28.4 billion in the high case.</p>

<div class="pdd-s4-dhead"><h3 class="pdd-s4-dname">Invoice coding</h3><span class="pdd-s4-badge">In production with vendor numbers</span><span class="pdd-s4-lever">Moves: Share of cost saved</span></div>
<dl class="pdd-s4-dl">
<dt class="pdd-label">Problem</dt><dd data-row="p"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="I#18" data-note="WEX restates a 2025 Ardent Partners benchmark. Best in class is $2.78.">$9.40</span> to process an average invoice, WEX reports.</dd>
<dt class="pdd-label">What AI does</dt><dd data-row="a">Reads the invoice and proposes coding.</dd>
<dt class="pdd-label">Stays with person or rule</dt><dd data-row="k"><span class="pdd-view">A person approves uncertain coding.</span></dd>
<dt class="pdd-label">Evidence and what cuts against it</dt><dd data-row="e">BILL: <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="H#10 H#13" data-note="BILL Invoice Coding Agent, February 2026. A vendor figure.">up to 89% fewer steps</span>.</span> Its own footnote: <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="H#13" data-note="BILL says results may vary.">one bill analysed</span>.</span></dd>
</dl>
</div>
</div>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-s4-right"><summary>Our arithmetic: market size and AI value</summary>
<div class="pdd-panel pdd-s4-range" data-m="flow">
<p class="pdd-label">AP automation market, 2026</p>
<ul class="pdd-s4-bars" aria-label="Three published sizes for the same market run from 3.8 to 6.57 billion dollars, 1.7 times apart">
<li style="--v:54.3%"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#2">$3.8B</span><span>Future Market Insights</span></li>
<li style="--v:91.4%"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#1">$6.4B</span><span>Research and Markets</span></li>
<li style="--v:93.9%"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#3" data-note="Its own table shows a conflicting growth rate.">$6.57B</span><span>The Business Research Company</span></li>
</ul>
<p class="pdd-s4-fine"><span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#2 J#3" data-note="Our arithmetic: 6.57 / 3.8 = 1.73.">1.7 times apart</span>.</span> These size a market, unlike the sum below.</p>
</div>
<div class="pdd-panel pdd-s4-calc">
<div class="pdd-s4-chead"><p class="pdd-label">Our arithmetic, illustration, United States</p><div class="pdd-ctl" role="group" aria-label="Load a worked case"><button type="button" class="pdd-pill" data-preset="a" aria-pressed="true">Default case</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill" data-preset="b" aria-pressed="false">High case</button></div></div>
<div class="pdd-s4-sliders">
<div class="pdd-s4-sl" data-k="pay" data-m="cost"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-s4-pay">Payments a year</label> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#24 N#3" data-note="Close to 8.1 billion B2B ACH payments in 2025, Nacha data. Cheques, wires and cards are outside. The slider range is an illustration. Q2 2026, all ACH: 9.3 billion payments worth $25.9 trillion.">B2B ACH, 2025</span></p><output for="pdd-s4-pay">8.1B</output><input type="range" id="pdd-s4-pay" min="1" max="12" step="0.1" value="8.1"></div>
<div class="pdd-s4-sl" data-k="cost" data-m="cost"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-s4-cost">Cost per item</label> <span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#26 I#19" data-note="WEX restates a $9.40 average and $2.78 best in class. Corpay cites $10.89. Applied per payment as an illustration.">Invoice cost, WEX reports</span></p><output for="pdd-s4-cost">$9.40</output><input type="range" id="pdd-s4-cost" min="2.78" max="10.89" step="0.01" value="9.4"></div>
<div class="pdd-s4-sl" data-k="auto" data-m="cost"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-s4-auto">Share automatable</label> <span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#26" data-note="The default is the 14% average invoice exception rate WEX restates. The range is an illustration.">Exception rate proxy</span></p><output for="pdd-s4-auto">14%</output><input type="range" id="pdd-s4-auto" min="5" max="100" step="1" value="14"></div>
<div class="pdd-s4-sl" data-k="save" data-m="cost"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-s4-save">Share of cost saved</label> <span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#26" data-note="Our arithmetic: the gap to best in class, 6.62 / 9.40.">70% ceiling</span></p><output for="pdd-s4-save">35%</output><input type="range" id="pdd-s4-save" min="5" max="70" step="5" value="35"></div>

Markers: about 1% was AI-linked, and the FBI froze 58% of attempted theft it acted on.">of $3.05B</span></p><output for="pdd-s4-fraud">25%</output><input type="range" id="pdd-s4-fraud" min="1" max="60" step="1" value="25"></div>

<div class="pdd-s4-bar" role="img" aria-label="Default case: processing value 3.73 billion dollars and fraud value 0.76 billion dollars a year, 4.5 billion in total"><i data-m="cost" data-seg="proc" style="width:12.4%"></i><i data-m="risk" data-seg="fraud" style="width:2.5%"></i></div>
<ul class="pdd-s4-leg"><li data-m="cost"><span class="pdd-dot"></span>Processing <b data-out="proc">$3.73B</b></li><li data-m="risk"><span class="pdd-dot"></span>Fraud <b data-out="fraud">$0.76B</b></li></ul>
</div>
</div>
</details>
<div class="pdd-panel pdd-pg" id="pdd-guard" data-m="risk" aria-label="Payee change guard">
<div class="pdd-pg-head"><h3>Payee change guard</h3><p class="pdd-pg-tl pdd-view">Rules catch, AI flags, a rule or a person releases.</p></div>
<ol class="pdd-pg-strip"><li class="pdd-pg-step" data-a="rule"><span class="pdd-pg-ico"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M14 22h52v36H14z"/><path d="M14 22l26 20 26-20"/></svg></span><span class="pdd-pg-n">1</span><b>Capture any channel</b><span class="pdd-pg-a">Rule</span><p>Email, portal, phone, ERP edits and new vendors all enter. Nothing goes live yet.</p></li><li class="pdd-pg-step" data-a="ai"><span class="pdd-pg-ico"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="16" y="12" width="36" height="50" rx="5"/><path d="M24 26h20M24 36h20M24 46h10"/><path d="M58 44l3 7 7 3-7 3-3 7-3-7-7-3 7-3z"/></svg></span><span class="pdd-pg-n">2</span><b>Read request</b><span class="pdd-pg-a">AI</span><p>Flags signals. It can only add a hold.</p></li><li class="pdd-pg-step" data-a="rule"><span class="pdd-pg-ico"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 34 38 16l26 18z"/><path d="M19 34v24M31 34v24M45 34v24M57 34v24M12 64h52"/></svg></span><span class="pdd-pg-n">3</span><b>Check account</b><span class="pdd-pg-a">Rule</span><p>Account owner, name, age, bank and country change.</p></li><li class="pdd-pg-step" data-a="ai"><span class="pdd-pg-ico"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 56a28 28 0 0 1 56 0"/><path d="M40 56l14-18"/><circle cx="40" cy="56" r="4"/></svg></span><span class="pdd-pg-n">4</span><b>Score risk</b><span class="pdd-pg-a">Rule and AI</span><p>First payment, unusual amount, recent contact change.</p></li><li class="pdd-pg-step" data-a="person"><span class="pdd-pg-ico"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M22 12h12l5 14-8 5a32 32 0 0 0 18 18l5-8 14 5v12c0 4-3 6-7 6C34 64 16 46 16 19c0-4 2-7 6-7z"/></svg></span><span class="pdd-pg-n">5</span><b>Verify out of band</b><span class="pdd-pg-a">Person</span><p>Call a number older than any change, or a public registry for new vendors.</p></li><li class="pdd-pg-step" data-a="person"><span class="pdd-pg-ico"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="18" y="34" width="44" height="32" rx="6"/><path d="M28 34V24a12 12 0 0 1 24 0v10"/><path d="M40 46v8"/></svg></span><span class="pdd-pg-n">6</span><b>Hold and release</b><span class="pdd-pg-a">Person</span><p>A different person releases. Maximum hold published.</p></li><li class="pdd-pg-step" data-a="person"><span class="pdd-pg-ico"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="40" cy="40" r="26"/><path d="M28 41l8 8 16-18"/></svg></span><span class="pdd-pg-n">7</span><b>Confirm receipt</b><span class="pdd-pg-a">Person</span><p>Confirm arrival via the old contact. If caught: freeze, recall, report.</p></li></ol>
<p class="pdd-pg-ev">No independent result yet for AI on this step. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="K#1" data-note="Baltimore 2025: vendor Workday account taken over. $1.52M sent, $721K recovered, $803K lost, insurance claim filed.">$1.5 million</span> Staff approved the change without checking documents. <span class="pdd-view">A callback stopping it is our assumption.</span></p>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-pg-more"><summary>How three personas broke it</summary><div class="pdd-pg-mw"><table class="pdd-pg-t"><thead><tr><th scope="col">Gap</th><th scope="col">Raised by</th><th scope="col">Fix</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Portal and ERP changes skip the email read</td><td>CFO, Investigator, Supplier</td><td>Every channel enters the guard. Nothing goes live before the callback.</td></tr><tr><td>The number on file was changed first, or the call is deepfaked</td><td>All three</td><td>Cooling period on contact changes. Confirm a code sent to a second channel.</td></tr><tr><td>One insider edits, calls back and releases</td><td>Investigator</td><td>Split the duties. Overrides need two named approvers.</td></tr><tr><td>A real but hacked mailbox, or a mule account in the same name, passes</td><td>Investigator</td><td>A clean read never lowers risk. Weight account age and bank change.</td></tr><tr><td>False positives hold honest suppliers</td><td>CFO, Supplier</td><td>Run in shadow mode first. Publish a maximum hold and show payees the status.</td></tr><tr><td>No owner, no evidence an insurer would accept</td><td>CFO, Investigator</td><td>Name one owner. Keep a record that shows how you meet the Nacha 2026 rule (ACH only).</td></tr><tr><td>Bulk vendor imports skip the guard</td><td>Investigator</td><td>Bulk imports go through the same checks. Phone agents cannot edit bank fields.</td></tr><tr><td>Slow-burn changes pass the first-payment hold</td><td>Investigator, CFO</td><td>Keep new details on probation. Confirm receipt through the old contact.</td></tr><tr><td>Honest edge cases: time zones, bank mergers, no name-check scheme</td><td>Supplier</td><td>Bookable callback slots. Treat "no scheme" as neutral.</td></tr></tbody></table><p class="pdd-pg-weak">Biggest weakness: the callback trusts a record the attacker may already have changed.</p><p class="pdd-pg-fine">Personas are reasoning roles, not interviews.</p></div></details>
</div>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-s4-cuts" data-m="risk"><summary>What cuts against it</summary>
<ul class="pdd-s4-tiles">
<li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#30" data-note="Gartner survey of 204 finance leaders, March 2026, as republished.">63%</span><span>slower AI rollout than expected, Gartner reports</span></li>
<li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#31" data-note="Sample size is not stated on the page.">35%</span><span>measure AI return well, Protiviti reports</span></li>
<li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="I#36" data-note="Finch benchmark. Every frontier system passed fewer than half.">38.4%</span><span>of accounting workflows passed, best agent</span></li>
<li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="I#29" data-note="Revised interagency guidance, April 17, 2026.">Out of scope</span><span>generative AI, in US model risk guidance</span></li>
</ul>
</details>
</div>

<section class="pdd-scene pdd-bd-scene" id="pdd-build" aria-labelledby="pdd-bd-q" data-tab="who">
<p class="pdd-kicker"><b>3</b>The build</p>
<h2 class="pdd-q" id="pdd-bd-q">How agent payments get built</h2>
<p class="pdd-a">Twelve steps carry one agent payment. Your payment core checks the mandate, a rule checks the payee, and a release matrix picks who releases each payment.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: what each party gains, and what to ask your bank.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: the AWS layout, the seven checks and the integrations.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">For you: who gains, who loses and the verdict.</span></p>
<div class="pdd-stage">
<div class="pdd-seg pdd-bd-seg" data-key="tab" role="group" aria-label="Choose a view of the build">
<button type="button" data-val="who" aria-pressed="true">Who gains</button>
<button type="button" data-val="loss" aria-pressed="false">Who loses</button>
<button type="button" data-val="arch" aria-pressed="false">Architecture</button>
<button type="button" data-val="flow" aria-pressed="false">Flow</button>
<button type="button" data-val="int" aria-pressed="false">Integrations</button>
<button type="button" data-val="plan" aria-pressed="false">Delivery</button>
<button type="button" data-val="verdict" aria-pressed="false">Verdict</button>
</div>
<div class="pdd-bd-p" data-p="who" role="region" aria-label="Who gains">
<div class="pdd-bd-cards">
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-card" data-m="bank"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><svg class="pdd-bd-icon" viewBox="0 0 80 80" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M10 30 40 12l30 18z"/><path d="M18 30v28M32 30v28M48 30v28M62 30v28M10 64h60"/><circle cx="40" cy="22" r="3"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Bank</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#18" data-note="Mastercard says every issuer in Europe is enabled for Agent Pay at network level, and 36 banks ran live transactions with passkeys.">36</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">banks in controlled consumer pilots, Europe</span><span class="pdd-tag">Vendor figure</span></p><dl class="pdd-bd-dl"><dt>Gains</dt><dd>Issues and verifies <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#19" data-note="Visa Agentic Ready: 20+ partners live in the UK and EU, 85+ planned in APAC and LatAm. Issuers test tokenization and authorization.">agent tokens</span>, and stays in every approval.</dd><dt>Risk</dt><dd>Still <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#38" data-note="A fraud vendor's opinion: issuers still retain liability and must show why they approved or declined an agent transaction.">carries the liability</span> and must explain each decision.</dd><dt>AI role</dt><dd>Scores <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#39" data-note="FIS says its issuer platform uses know-your-agent data to authorize agent payments. A vendor claim.">know-your-agent data</span> when it authorizes.</dd></dl></article>
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-card" data-m="flow"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><svg class="pdd-bd-icon" viewBox="0 0 80 80" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><rect x="12" y="26" width="56" height="38" rx="5"/><path d="M30 26v-8h20v8"/><path d="M12 42h56"/><path d="M36 42v6h8v-6"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Business payer</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#7" data-note="AFP 2026 survey: 76% of firms had payments fraud in 2025, 74% were hit by email compromise, and 17% use AI against fraud.">74%</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">of firms hit by email compromise, 2025</span></p><dl class="pdd-bd-dl"><dt>Gains</dt><dd>Payment runs without keying, as <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#32" data-note="Coupa says its payment batch agent ran 2,395 payments worth $20.1M in 5 weeks at one customer. A vendor claim.">Coupa reports</span>.</dd><dt>Risk</dt><dd>The loss stays with the payer if the bank <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#6" data-note="UCC 4A-202(b): an order counts as the customer's whether or not authorized, if the agreed security procedure was commercially reasonable and the bank followed it in good faith.">followed the procedure</span>.</dd><dt>AI role</dt><dd>Matches invoices, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#31" data-note="Oracle Payables and Payments agents cover intake, matching, fraud checks, early pay, cards and bank execution. Oracle, October 2025. A vendor claim.">checks fraud</span> and proposes the run.</dd></dl></article>
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-card" data-m="ok"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><svg class="pdd-bd-icon" viewBox="0 0 80 80" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M12 32h56v32H12z"/><path d="M8 32l8-16h48l8 16"/><path d="M32 64V46h16v18"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Supplier</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#8" data-note="Billtrust reports 20M+ buyers and $1T of trade credit. Its own figure.">$1T</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">trade credit on Billtrust</span><span class="pdd-tag">Vendor figure</span></p><dl class="pdd-bd-dl"><dt>Gains</dt><dd>Paid through its portal with <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#8" data-note="Visa, BILL and Billtrust pilot, October 20, 2026: agents pay approved invoices through supplier portals.">no CAPTCHA or shared logins</span>.</dd><dt>Risk</dt><dd>Fraudsters pose as the supplier and <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#5" data-note="Nacha's False Pretenses covers email compromise, vendor impersonation and payroll impersonation.">redirect</span> its money.</dd><dt>AI role</dt><dd>Checks the <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#22" data-note="Trusted Agent Protocol: the merchant checks the agent's RFC 9421 signature against the agent's public key.">agent's signature</span> before it accepts.</dd></dl></article>
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-card" data-m="coin"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><svg class="pdd-bd-icon" viewBox="0 0 80 80" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><circle cx="40" cy="40" r="8"/><circle cx="14" cy="18" r="6"/><circle cx="66" cy="18" r="6"/><circle cx="14" cy="62" r="6"/><circle cx="66" cy="62" r="6"/><path d="M19 22l15 12M61 22 46 34M19 58l15-12M61 58 46 46"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Network or processor</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#19" data-note="Visa Agentic Ready: 20+ partners live in the UK and EU, 85+ planned in APAC and LatAm.">20+</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">Visa partners live in the UK and EU</span><span class="pdd-tag">Vendor figure</span></p><dl class="pdd-bd-dl"><dt>Gains</dt><dd>Runs the agent <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#21" data-note="Visa TAP and Mastercard Agent Pay both use Web Bot Auth, with key directories run by Visa and Mastercard. Cloudflare, October 2025.">key directory</span> and the token layer.</dd><dt>Risk</dt><dd>The Visa B2B pilot has <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#9" data-note="No liability framework, and no disclosed volumes, participants or general availability date.">no liability framework</span>.</dd><dt>AI role</dt><dd>Binds a token to <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#18" data-note="Mastercard Agent Pay issues agentic tokens for each agent.">each agent</span> and checks its use.</dd></dl></article>
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-card" data-m="rule"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><svg class="pdd-bd-icon" viewBox="0 0 80 80" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><rect x="18" y="26" width="44" height="38" rx="9"/><path d="M40 26V15"/><circle cx="40" cy="11" r="4"/><circle cx="31" cy="42" r="4"/><circle cx="49" cy="42" r="4"/><path d="M31 54h18M10 40v10M70 40v10"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Agent platform</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#8" data-note="BILL serves about 500,000 businesses. Its own figure.">500K</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">businesses on BILL</span><span class="pdd-tag">Vendor figure</span></p><dl class="pdd-bd-dl"><dt>Gains</dt><dd>Moves from automating payables to <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#8" data-note="Visa calls it the first live pilot through supplier portals, October 20, 2026. Visa&#39;s own claim.">executing</span> the payments.</dd><dt>Risk</dt><dd>Large banks <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#10" data-note="JPMorgan, Citi, BofA and Schwab will not let outside agents move money. JPMorgan allows read-only access through Plaid. The report covers consumer accounts.">block outside agents</span> on consumer accounts.</dd><dt>AI role</dt><dd><span class="pdd-view">Runs the payment inside the mandate.</span></dd></dl></article>
</div>
</div>
<div class="pdd-bd-p" data-p="loss" role="region" aria-label="Who loses">
<div class="pdd-wl">
<div class="pdd-panel pdd-wl-heat"><div class="pdd-wl-hh"><p class="pdd-label pdd-view">Money at risk against power to fix, scored 1 to 5</p><p class="pdd-wl-say">The payer loses most. The bank can fix most.</p><p class="pdd-wl-key"><i data-k="risk"></i>Money at risk <i data-k="fix"></i>Power to fix</p></div><div class="pdd-wl-grid" role="group" aria-label="Pick a party to highlight"><button type="button" class="pdd-wl-col" data-party="payer" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Business payer: money at risk 5 of 5, power to fix 3 of 5"><span class="pdd-wl-nm">Payer</span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="risk" style="--v:5"><i></i><b>5</b></span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="fix" style="--v:3"><i></i><b>3</b></span></button><button type="button" class="pdd-wl-col" data-party="supplier" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Supplier: money at risk 4 of 5, power to fix 1 of 5"><span class="pdd-wl-nm">Supplier</span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="risk" style="--v:4"><i></i><b>4</b></span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="fix" style="--v:1"><i></i><b>1</b></span></button><button type="button" class="pdd-wl-col" data-party="bank" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Bank: money at risk 3 of 5, power to fix 5 of 5"><span class="pdd-wl-nm">Bank</span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="risk" style="--v:3"><i></i><b>3</b></span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="fix" style="--v:5"><i></i><b>5</b></span></button><button type="button" class="pdd-wl-col" data-party="network" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Network or processor: money at risk 2 of 5, power to fix 4 of 5"><span class="pdd-wl-nm">Network</span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="risk" style="--v:2"><i></i><b>2</b></span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="fix" style="--v:4"><i></i><b>4</b></span></button><button type="button" class="pdd-wl-col" data-party="platform" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Agent platform: money at risk 1 of 5, power to fix 4 of 5"><span class="pdd-wl-nm">Platform</span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="risk" style="--v:1"><i></i><b>1</b></span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="fix" style="--v:4"><i></i><b>4</b></span></button></div></div>
<ol class="pdd-wl-cards"><li class="pdd-panel pdd-wl-card" data-party="payer" data-m="cost"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><span class="pdd-wl-rk">1</span><svg class="pdd-bd-icon" viewBox="0 0 80 80" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><rect x="12" y="26" width="56" height="38" rx="5"/><path d="M30 26v-8h20v8"/><path d="M12 42h56"/><path d="M36 42v6h8v-6"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Business payer</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="L#1" data-note="FBI IC3 2025: business email compromise losses reported, all channels.">$3.05B</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">email fraud losses, 2025</span></p><p class="pdd-wl-fx"><b>Who fixes</b> <span class="pdd-view">Owns the payee master data and the mandate it signs. Only it knows the real supplier.</span></p><details class="pdd-more pdd-wl-ev"><summary>Evidence</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Bound under <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#6">UCC 4A-202</span> when the bank followed procedure.</li><li>Courts can make it <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#32" data-note="Foley Hoag, April 3, 2026: most courts put BEC losses on the party best placed to prevent the fraud. In one 2024 case the payer still owed the agreed amount.">pay the supplier again</span>.</li><li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="I#7">74%</span> of organizations were hit by email fraud in 2025.</li><li>Losses: <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#7" data-note="Children's Healthcare of Atlanta, June 2023: a hacker posing as a vendor changed its ACH instructions.">$5.3M</span> at a children's hospital group, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="K#1">$803K</span> never recovered in Baltimore, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#9" data-note="Arup, January 2024: a Hong Kong employee joined a video call with deepfaked colleagues.">$25.6M</span> at Arup.</li><li>The UK CMA says a business answers for its agent as for an <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#3" data-note="Reported guidance of March 9, 2026.">employee</span>. Customers who share credentials with agents <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#11">could be responsible</span>.</li></ul></details></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-wl-card" data-party="supplier" data-m="flow"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><span class="pdd-wl-rk">2</span><svg class="pdd-bd-icon" viewBox="0 0 80 80" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M12 32h56v32H12z"/><path d="M8 32l8-16h48l8 16"/><path d="M32 64V46h16v18"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Supplier</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#4" data-note="Chargeflow cites a Mastercard article with Datos Insights research: merchant chargeback costs of $36.9 billion in 2026, rising to $46.1 billion in 2029. A forecast.">$36.9B</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">merchant chargebacks, 2026 forecast</span></p><p class="pdd-wl-fx"><b>Who fixes</b> <span class="pdd-view">Has little power to fix it. Gains when banks check <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#35 N#36">payee names</span>.</span></p><details class="pdd-more pdd-wl-ev"><summary>Evidence</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>In card flows the merchant <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#4">generally carries</span> chargebacks.</li><li>In push payments it goes unpaid while <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#32">courts split</span>.</li><li>One firm says <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#8" data-note="Frontline Fabrics sued its insurer and banks on December 26, 2025. The claims are allegations only.">$1.43M</span> meant for its supplier went to a new account. An allegation.</li></ul></details></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-wl-card" data-party="bank" data-m="bank"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><span class="pdd-wl-rk">3</span><svg class="pdd-bd-icon" viewBox="0 0 80 80" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M10 30 40 12l30 18z"/><path d="M18 30v28M32 30v28M48 30v28M62 30v28M10 64h60"/><circle cx="40" cy="22" r="3"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Bank</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#4 R#10 R#11" data-note="UK mandatory APP fraud reimbursement since October 7, 2024 covers consumers, micro firms and small charities. The PSR cap per claim converted at the Bank of England rate of October 22, 2026. Larger firms fall outside it.">$114K</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">about $114,000 (UK cap) per refund claim</span><span class="pdd-tag" data-k="calc">Our arithmetic</span></p><p class="pdd-wl-fx"><b>Who fixes</b> <span class="pdd-view">Owns the security procedure and the duty to monitor.</span></p><details class="pdd-more pdd-wl-ev"><summary>Evidence</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Carries the loss when its procedure was not <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#6">commercially reasonable</span> or not followed.</li><li>Must run Nacha <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#4 N#5">fraud monitoring</span>.</li><li>Gets <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#8">sued</span> when a payment goes astray.</li><li>Large banks <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#10">block outside agents</span> on consumer accounts.</li></ul></details></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-wl-card" data-party="network" data-m="coin"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><span class="pdd-wl-rk">4</span><svg class="pdd-bd-icon" viewBox="0 0 80 80" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><circle cx="40" cy="40" r="8"/><circle cx="14" cy="18" r="6"/><circle cx="66" cy="18" r="6"/><circle cx="14" cy="62" r="6"/><circle cx="66" cy="62" r="6"/><path d="M19 22l15 12M61 22 46 34M19 58l15-12M61 58 46 46"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Network or processor</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#11" data-note="Amex Agent Purchase Protection, April 2026: covers agent errors when the agent is registered and the customer's intent is authenticated.">Amex</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">covers registered agent errors</span></p><p class="pdd-wl-fx"><b>Who fixes</b> <span class="pdd-view">Owns the dispute rules and <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#14 N#23">agent registration</span>.</span></p><details class="pdd-more pdd-wl-ev"><summary>Evidence</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Merchants and issuers carry <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#4 N#38">most losses</span>.</li><li>Amex covers agent errors for <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#11">registered agents</span>.</li></ul></details></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-wl-card" data-party="platform" data-m="rule"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><span class="pdd-wl-rk">5</span><svg class="pdd-bd-icon" viewBox="0 0 80 80" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><rect x="18" y="26" width="44" height="38" rx="9"/><path d="M40 26V15"/><circle cx="40" cy="11" r="4"/><circle cx="31" cy="42" r="4"/><circle cx="49" cy="42" r="4"/><path d="M31 54h18M10 40v10M70 40v10"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Agent platform</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#9" data-note="The Visa, BILL and Billtrust pilot sets no formal liability framework.">None</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">liability rule for agents yet</span></p><p class="pdd-wl-fx"><b>Who fixes</b> <span class="pdd-view">Owns the mandate scope: <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#1 N#34 O#28 O#14">caps, expiry and revocation</span>.</span></p><details class="pdd-more pdd-wl-ev"><summary>Evidence</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>The Visa B2B pilot sets <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#9">no liability framework</span>.</li><li><span class="pdd-view">No rule yet assigns fault to an agent.</span></li></ul></details></li></ol>
</div>
</div>

only</span></li><li><svg width="22" height="22" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M11 1 21 11 11 21 1 11z" fill="#e0a030"/></svg><span>SOX key control</span></li><li><svg width="22" height="22" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="9" class="pdd-ar3-lport"/></svg><span>Portable</span></li><li><svg width="22" height="22" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="2" y="2" width="18" height="18" rx="3" fill="#ff9900"/></svg><span>AWS-bound</span></li></ul></details></div>
<p class="pdd-ar3-out" aria-live="polite">Press Play to run the story through this stage.</p>
<ol class="pdd-ar3-steps" data-st="today" aria-label="Six steps today">
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-c1" data-s="1" data-m="flow"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">1</b><span>Invoice by email</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>Supplier invoices, and any bank detail change, arrive by email.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt data-k="pain">Pain</dt><dd><b class="pdd-ar3-pn">1</b> <b>Spoofable.</b> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#7" data-note="AFP 2026 survey: 74% of firms were hit by email compromise in 2025.">74%</span> of firms were hit by email compromise in 2025.</dd><dt>Target</dt><dd>The agent drafts only from an approved invoice, step 2.</dd></dl></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-c2" data-s="2" data-m="flow"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">2</b><span>AP clerk in ERP</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>A clerk keys the invoice and the payee into the ERP.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Cost</dt><dd>About <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#26 I#19" data-note="WEX restates a $9.40 average invoice cost and $2.78 best in class.">$9.40</span> to process one invoice.</dd><dt>Target</dt><dd>Invoice match checks the invoice of record, step 5.</dd></dl></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-c3" data-s="3" data-m="risk"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">3</b><span>Manual payee check</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>Bank detail changes are checked by a phone call-back.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt data-k="pain">Pain</dt><dd><b class="pdd-ar3-pn">2</b> <b>Skipped checks.</b> Call-backs slip under deadline. Baltimore lost <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="K#1">$803K</span> to a changed vendor account.</dd><dt>Target</dt><dd>A rule checks every payment, step 5.</dd></dl></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-c4" data-s="4" data-m="bank"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">4</b><span>Bank portal</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>The payment is keyed again into the bank portal. A maker and a checker approve it there.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt data-k="pain">Pain</dt><dd><b class="pdd-ar3-pn">3</b> <b>Re-keying.</b> Errors, and no single audit trail.</dd><dt data-k="pain">Pain</dt><dd><b class="pdd-ar3-pn">4</b> <b>Blind approval.</b> The checker sees the amount, not the payee source.</dd><dt>Target</dt><dd>The checker sees the guard result, step 8.</dd></dl></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-c5" data-s="5" data-m="bank"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">5</b><span>ACH or wire</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>Batch ACH, or a same-day wire.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt data-k="pain">Pain</dt><dd><b class="pdd-ar3-pn">5</b> <b>Batch rails.</b> Cut-offs, and no after-hours payouts.</dd><dt>Target</dt><dd>RTP and FedNow run 24/7 up to <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#37 O#33">$10M</span>, step 9.</dd></dl></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-c6" data-s="6" data-m="ok"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">6</b><span>Reconcile</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>The bank statement is matched by hand in a spreadsheet.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt data-k="pain">Pain</dt><dd><b class="pdd-ar3-pn">6</b> <b>Slow close.</b> Evidence spreads across inboxes.</dd><dt>Target</dt><dd>Returns and status come back to a ledger and the ERP, step 11.</dd></dl></div></details></li>
</ol>
<ol class="pdd-ar3-steps" data-st="year1" aria-label="Nine steps in Year 1">
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-y1" data-s="1" data-m="ai"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">1</b><span>Agent proposes</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>The agent drafts a payout from an approved invoice. It holds no release tool.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Input</dt><dd>Invoice email and the ERP, both untrusted</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>A bank detail found in a document goes to the payee guard, step 3.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-y2" data-s="2" data-m="coin"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">2</b><span>Mandate check</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="port">Portable</span></p><p>Inside the thin service, it checks the platform signature against the public key and the passkey mandate against the key stored at enrolment. The adapter never checks itself.</p><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>A signing proxy adds <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#21 N#22" data-note="Web Bot Auth: HTTP Message Signatures (RFC 9421) with a nonce. Visa Trusted Agent Protocol, Mastercard Agent Pay and Amex use it.">Web Bot Auth</span> signatures to calls out to card networks.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-y3" data-s="3" data-m="risk"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">3</b><span>Payee guard</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span></p><p><span class="pdd-view">A rule, not a model, checks every payment.</span></p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Checks</dt><dd>Invoice match, sanctions screen, US account check</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Invoice match reads the approved invoice from the ERP and checks amount, payee and not yet paid.</li><li>The sanctions screen runs every party against the SDN list feed. A hit goes to a queue for a person.</li><li>Baltimore approved a <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="K#1">changed vendor account</span> without checking it.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-y4" data-s="4" data-m="bank"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">4</b><span>Rail picker</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>Picks ACH, RTP or wire before the release decision, and writes the rail into the release record.</p><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>RTP and FedNow take up to <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#37 O#33" data-note="RTP raised its limit to $10M on February 9, 2025. FedNow raised its limit to $10M in November 2025.">$10M</span> per payment.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-y5" data-s="5" data-m="rule"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">5</b><span>Release matrix</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="port">Portable</span></p><p>Payee status crossed with amount and rail. A rule releases inside the matrix, a person outside it. The release is signed and takes a one-time key.</p><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>A retry checks the payment status first, so a timeout cannot pay twice.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-y6" data-s="6" data-m="ok"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">6</b><span>Checker app</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span></p><p>A person releases anything outside the matrix. They see the payee source and the guard result, then confirm with a passkey.</p></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-y7" data-s="7" data-m="bank"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">7</b><span>Bank connector</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="port">Portable</span></p><p>One adapter in front of the bank API you already have. It checks the signed release before it sends.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Lock</dt><dd>An explicit deny on the adapter and the bank secret blocks every principal except the release role.</dd></dl></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-y8" data-s="8" data-m="ok"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">8</b><span>Evidence ledger</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="port">Portable</span></p><p>Acknowledgements, returns and statements come back through the adapter and the event bus. Paid status goes to the ERP. A return opens an exception for a person.</p></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-y9" data-s="9" data-m="risk"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">9</b><span>Kill switch</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>Disable one agent, or switch all agent payments off. It takes effect within a minute, and the adapter checks the flag on every send.</p></div></details></li>
</ol>
<ol class="pdd-ar3-steps" data-st="target" aria-label="Steps on the target payment path">
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t1" data-s="1" data-m="coin"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">1</b><span>Key registration</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>The platform key is registered in a scheme directory. Only the signing proxy may sign with it. The proxy adds RFC 9421 signatures to calls out to supplier portals and card networks.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Signs</dt><dd>Signing proxy, with the platform key</dd><dt>Checks</dt><dd>The supplier portal or the card network, against the directory</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span> KMS holds an Ed25519 key. The proxy runs in Lambda.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>BILL registered its Payment Agent this way for the <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#8" data-note="Visa, BILL and Billtrust pilot, October 20, 2026. BILL&#39;s Payment Agent is registered through Trusted Agent Protocol.">Visa pilot</span>.</li><li>Visa TAP, Mastercard Agent Pay and Amex use <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#21 N#22" data-note="Web Bot Auth: HTTP Message Signatures (RFC 9421) with a nonce. Visa Trusted Agent Protocol, Mastercard Agent Pay and Amex use it.">Web Bot Auth</span>, so one proxy covers all three.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t2" data-s="2" data-m="ai"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">2</b><span>Agent and gateway</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="aws">AWS-bound</span></p><p>The agent drafts the payout from an approved invoice. Invoice PDFs, email and portals are untrusted input.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Role</dt><dd>Agent runtime and tool gateway</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#14" data-note="AgentCore Runtime is a serverless hosting environment for agents and tools, with session isolation and built-in identity.">AgentCore Runtime</span> hosts the agent. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#15" data-note="AgentCore Policy checks each tool call made through AgentCore Gateway, and can require an approval before a transfer.">AgentCore Policy</span> on the Gateway denies vendor master writes and release tools.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>A bank detail found in a document goes to the payee guard, step 5.</li><li>Ramp says its AP agents <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#26" data-note="Ramp Agents for AP, October 7, 2025: early access users flagged more than $1M of fraudulent invoices in 90 days. A vendor claim.">flagged over $1M</span> of fraudulent invoices in 90 days. <span class="pdd-tag">Vendor claim</span></li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t3" data-s="3" data-m="flow"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">3</b><span>Signed request</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>The request carries the platform signature and the signed mandate. The gateway calls with its own IAM role: Gateway role only.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Role</dt><dd>Payment API</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span> A public <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#12" data-note="Amazon API Gateway creates, publishes, monitors and secures REST, HTTP and WebSocket APIs.">API Gateway</span> behind an OpenAPI target. The resource policy names the gateway role.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Mandate layer: <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#15" data-note="AP2 v0.2 signs checkout and payment mandates, supports payments where the human is not present, and is moving to FIDO.">AP2</span> and <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#24" data-note="Mastercard Verifiable Intent, open-sourced and co-developed with Google, uses selective disclosure.">Verifiable Intent</span>, moving to FIDO.</li><li>A <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#25" data-note="Skyfire KYAPay, an individual IETF draft. The issuer does KYC or KYB, and recipients verify against its keys.">know-your-agent token</span> names the agent, the platform and the human.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t4" data-s="4" data-m="coin"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">4</b><span>Mandate check</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="port">Portable</span></p><p>Checks the platform signature against the KMS public key, Ed25519 under RFC 9421, and the passkey mandate against the key stored at enrolment. No directory round trip.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Owner</dt><dd>You, in the payment core</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#13" data-note="AWS Lambda runs code without provisioning or managing servers.">Lambda</span> checks. DynamoDB holds the keys, a nonce with a conditional write, and the revocation list.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Created and expires are enforced, and a nonce blocks replay. A key mismatch is rejected.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t5" data-s="5" data-m="risk"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">5</b><span>Payee guard</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span></p><p><span class="pdd-view">A rule, not a model, checks every payment.</span></p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Checks</dt><dd>Invoice match, sanctions screen, US account check</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#13" data-note="AWS Lambda runs code without provisioning or managing servers.">Lambda</span> for each check, and a payee registry in DynamoDB.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Invoice match reads the approved invoice from the ERP and checks amount, payee and not yet paid.</li><li>The sanctions screen runs every party against the SDN list feed. A hit goes to a queue for a person. See <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="H#31">sanctions</span>.</li><li>No scheme answer means a callback for a new or changed payee, never a neutral score. Other rules: limits and a <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#35 N#36" data-note="EU Verification of Payee since October 9, 2025. UK Confirmation of Payee covers over 99% of Faster Payments senders, Pay.UK, June 2025.">payee name check</span>.</li><li>Baltimore approved a <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="K#1">changed vendor account</span> without checking it.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t6" data-s="6" data-m="rule"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">6</b><span>Rail and policy</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="port">Portable</span></p><p>The rail picker chooses the rail first. Then the release policy judges payee status, amount and rail together. <span class="pdd-view">No LLM on approval path. ML score can only add a hold.</span></p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Role</dt><dd>Rail picker, release matrix and risk score</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span> A Lambda picks the rail. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#19" data-note="Amazon Verified Permissions decides with Cedar policies and assumes the caller was authenticated elsewhere.">Verified Permissions</span> runs the matrix as Cedar rules. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#42" data-note="Amazon SageMaker AI builds, trains and deploys ML models.">SageMaker AI</span> scores. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#17" data-note="Amazon Fraud Detector stopped taking new customers on November 7, 2025.">Fraud Detector</span> takes no new customers.</dd></dl><div class="pdd-rm pdd-rm-dr"><p class="pdd-rm-h"><b>Release matrix</b> <span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span> <span class="pdd-view">Bands are your own limits. RTP and FedNow use a lower band. An ML score can move a cell to Person, never back.</span></p><div class="pdd-rm-sc" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Release matrix, scroll sideways"><table class="pdd-rm-t"><thead><tr><th scope="col" rowspan="2">Payee</th><th scope="colgroup" colspan="2">ACH</th><th scope="colgroup" colspan="2">RTP and FedNow</th><th scope="colgroup" colspan="2">Card token</th><th scope="colgroup" colspan="2">Cross-border</th></tr><tr><th scope="col">Low</th><th scope="col">High</th><th scope="col">Low</th><th scope="col">High</th><th scope="col">Low</th><th scope="col">High</th><th scope="col">Low</th><th scope="col">High</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><th scope="row">Established</th><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="rule">Rule</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="rule">Rule</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="rule">Rule</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td></tr><tr><th scope="row">New</th><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td></tr><tr><th scope="row">Changed</th><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>The rail goes into the release record. If failover moves to a stricter rail, the checker approves again.</li><li>A new payee fails closed, so a person releases.</li><li>A policy change needs two approvers and a ticket.</li><li>Model risk falls under <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="S#1" data-note="Fed, OCC and FDIC replaced SR 11-7 with principles-based SR 26-2 on April 17, 2026.">SR 26-2</span>, which replaced SR 11-7 in April 2026. <span class="pdd-view">Generative AI needs its own control set.</span></li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t7" data-s="7" data-m="rule"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">7</b><span>Release</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="aws">AWS-bound</span></p><p>Two exits. Inside the matrix a rule releases. Outside it the run waits for a person, step 8. Either way, release signs an assertion that the bank adapter checks.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Signs</dt><dd>Payment ID, amount, payee hash, rail, matrix cell and approver</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#23" data-note="Step Functions Standard workflows support Wait for Callback with a task token, so a run pauses until the token returns.">Step Functions</span> Standard, waiting on a task token, with the payment ID as the execution name. A DynamoDB conditional write keeps a one-time key.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>An SCP on the payment core account denies SendTaskSuccess to the agent and orchestrator roles, so neither can approve itself.</li><li>A retry checks the payment status first, so a timeout cannot pay twice.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t8" data-s="8" data-m="ok"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">8</b><span>Person releases</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span></p><p>A checker in the checker account sees the payee source and the guard result, then approves with a passkey step-up.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Role</dt><dd>Checker app</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span> Cognito sign-in. The checker app assumes a release role in the payment core account to approve.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Controls modelled on <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#12" data-note="JPMorganChase plans for high agent autonomy in treasury, with controls modelled on maker-checker rules.">maker-checker</span> rules.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t9" data-s="9" data-m="bank"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">9</b><span>Bank rail</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="port">Portable</span></p><p>The adapter checks the signed release, then sends ACH or RTP through your own bank API and one controlled exit.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Lock</dt><dd>An explicit deny on the adapter and the bank secret blocks every principal except the release role.</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span> KMS Verify on the signed release. Lambda adapters in private subnets across two AZs exit through a Network Firewall endpoint and a NAT gateway with an allowlisted Elastic IP. Secrets Manager holds the mTLS bank credential.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Three ways to move money. Your own bank API makes you the Originator. A sponsor bank makes a startup a TPSP under <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#5" data-note="Nacha fraud monitoring phase 2 took effect June 19, 2026 and covers Originators, TPSPs and TPSs.">Nacha phase 2</span>. A card partner issues the card.</li><li>RTP and FedNow take up to <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#37 O#33" data-note="RTP raised its limit to $10M on February 9, 2025. FedNow raised its limit to $10M in November 2025.">$10M</span> per payment.</li><li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#13 O#14" data-note="AWS made AgentCore payments generally available on August 18, 2026: x402, MPP, and Coinbase or Stripe Privy wallets, with session budgets. AWS docs describe it for microtransactions, often under $1.">AgentCore payments</span> suits agent microtransactions, not supplier invoices.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t9b" data-s="9b" data-m="coin"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">9b</b><span>Stablecoin lane</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>Off until 2027. A Cedar policy gate in the payment core, then out through the same firewall.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Opens</dt><dd><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2 O#31" data-note="The OCC final rule and the GENIUS Act take effect on January 18, 2027. Regulators missed the July 2026 deadline for final rules.">January 18, 2027</span> at the earliest</dd><dt>Wins</dt><dd>Cross-border and after-hours payouts</dd><dt>Loses</dt><dd>Domestic dollars, where RTP and FedNow run 24/7</dd></dl><p class="pdd-ar3-gh">Policy gate, all four</p><ol class="pdd-vd-gates"><li><span class="pdd-vd-jn">1</span><span>A coin from a permitted issuer. The <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2 O#31" data-note="The OCC final rule for payment stablecoin issuers takes effect on January 18, 2027. It binds issuers, not payers.">OCC rule</span> binds issuers, not payers</span></li><li><span class="pdd-vd-jn">2</span><span>The <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2" data-note="AML and sanctions requirements come in a separate Treasury rulemaking, still to come.">Treasury AML and sanctions rule</span>, with a wallet screen</span></li><li><span class="pdd-vd-jn">3</span><span>A corridor price win. One test found <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#21" data-note="Banca d&#39;Italia sent 200 USDC across ten corridors in March 2026 and found no systematic advantage over traditional channels.">no systematic edge</span></span></li><li><span class="pdd-vd-jn">4</span><span>Payee consent</span></li></ol><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#8" data-note="Ledger Insights, October 1, 2026: Open USD went live about September 30, 2026, with Coinbase, Mastercard, Shopify, Stripe and Visa as founding investors. A secondary report.">Open USD</span> went live on September 30, 2026.</li><li>A bank option for the same leg: four big US banks plan a <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#9" data-note="The Paypers citing the WSJ, June 5, 2026: JPMorgan Chase, Bank of America, Citigroup and Wells Fargo plan a shared tokenized deposit network for the first half of 2027. A secondary report.">24/7 tokenized deposit network</span> for 2027.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t10" data-s="10" data-m="flow"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">10</b><span>Card lane</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>Secondary and pilot-ready. The agent handles only network tokens or single-use card references.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Owner</dt><dd>Card-issuing partner</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span> A card fill Lambda in an isolated CDE subnet. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#20" data-note="Managed payment HSM functions designed for PCI PIN, PCI P2PE and PCI DSS.">Payment Cryptography</span> holds card keys. No PIN keys unless you issue cards.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Mastercard Agent Pay is live for <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#18" data-note="June 2, 2026: every Mastercard issuer in Europe is enabled for Agent Pay at network level, and 36 banks completed controlled live payments with passkeys. Consumer payments.">European consumers</span> only.</li><li>The Visa B2B pilot shows <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#9" data-note="No liability framework, and no disclosed volumes, participants or general availability date.">no volumes</span> yet.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t11" data-s="11" data-m="ok"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">11</b><span>Ledger and returns</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="port">Portable</span></p><p>Returns and status come back. A bank webhook or statement reaches the adapter, then the event bus, the ledger and the ERP. A return opens an exception for a person.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Role</dt><dd>Ledger and audit store</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#36" data-note="EventBridge custom buses support retention, ordered delivery and replay.">EventBridge</span> feeds a DynamoDB ledger, then <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#22" data-note="S3 Object Lock stores objects write once, read many, assessed for SEC 17a-4, CFTC and FINRA.">S3 Object Lock</span> in compliance mode in the log archive account. An <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#40" data-note="AWS CloudTrail records actions by users, roles and services as events for auditing.">organization trail</span> logs every call.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Evidence pack: payment ID, mandate hash, key ID, guard result, matrix cell, approver and rail reference.</li><li>Agent traces carry the payment ID. Agent-originated ACH entries are tagged for <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#4">Nacha monitoring</span>.</li><li>Four accounts under Organizations with SCPs: agent runtime, payment core, checker and log archive.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t12" data-s="12" data-m="risk"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">12</b><span>Kill switch</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>Disable one agent, switch all agent payments off, or revoke the key in the directories. It takes effect within a minute.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Role</dt><dd>Control plane</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span> AppConfig holds the flag, and the adapter reads it on every send. Systems Manager runs the recall playbook.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Recall: an ACH <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#5" data-note="A receiving bank can return a suspect credit with R17.">R17 return</span>, a wire recall, or an RTP request for return.</li></ul></div></details></li>
</ol>
</div>
</div>
<div class="pdd-bd-p" data-p="flow" role="region" aria-label="Flow">
<div class="pdd-bd-fhead"><p class="pdd-label">One agent-made supplier payment</p><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-bd-run">Run the payment</button><span class="pdd-bd-fst" role="status" aria-live="polite"></span></div>
<ol class="pdd-bd-steps">
<li class="pdd-bd-step" data-m="flow"><div class="pdd-bd-sh"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><rect x="12" y="6" width="24" height="36" rx="3"/><path d="M18 16h12M18 23h12M18 30h7"/></svg></span><b>1 Intent</b></div><p><i>Fails</i> No approved invoice behind it</p><p><i>Caught by</i> A <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#8" data-note="The Visa, BILL and Billtrust pilot ties controls to the buyer's approval.">mandate</span> bound to the invoice</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="rule">Rule</span></li>
<li class="pdd-bd-step" data-m="coin"><div class="pdd-bd-sh"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><rect x="8" y="14" width="32" height="22" rx="4"/><circle cx="18" cy="25" r="4"/><path d="M26 22h8M26 28h6"/></svg></span><b>2 Mandate check</b></div><p><i>Fails</i> Amount or payee outside the mandate</p><p><i>Caught by</i> Key signature, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#34 N#25" data-note="Mercury: the agent cannot change its card limits. KYAPay: a token names the agent, the platform and the human.">locked limits</span></p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="bank">Bank</span></li>
<li class="pdd-bd-step" data-m="risk"><div class="pdd-bd-sh"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><circle cx="24" cy="16" r="8"/><path d="M9 42c2-9 8-13 15-13s13 4 15 13"/></svg></span><b>3 Payee guard</b></div><p><i>Fails</i> Bank details changed or name mismatch</p><p><i>Caught by</i> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#35 N#36">Name check</span>, then a callback</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="person">Rule, person</span></li>
<li class="pdd-bd-step" data-m="risk"><div class="pdd-bd-sh"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M8 34a16 16 0 0 1 32 0"/><path d="M24 34l8-10"/><circle cx="24" cy="34" r="3"/></svg></span><b>4 AI risk score</b></div><p><i>Fails</i> An <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#7" data-note="74% of firms were hit by email compromise in 2025, AFP reports.">email compromise</span> pattern slips by</p><p><i>Caught by</i> <span class="pdd-view">ML score can only add a hold</span></p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="ai">AI</span></li>
<li class="pdd-bd-step" data-m="rule"><div class="pdd-bd-sh"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><rect x="11" y="22" width="26" height="20" rx="4"/><path d="M17 22v-6a7 7 0 0 1 14 0v6"/><path d="M24 30v4"/></svg></span><b>5 Release matrix</b></div><p><i>Fails</i> The agent releases alone</p><p><i>Caught by</i> A rule for known payees in band, a person for the rest, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#12 N#10">maker-checker</span></p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="person">Rule or person</span></li>
<li class="pdd-bd-step" data-m="bank"><div class="pdd-bd-sh"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M6 24h22M18 24c8 0 6-14 16-14h8M18 24c8 0 6 14 16 14h8"/></svg></span><b>6 Send</b></div><p><i>Fails</i> Sent twice, or over the <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#37" data-note="RTP takes up to $10M per payment since February 9, 2025.">rail limit</span></p><p><i>Caught by</i> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#29">Idempotency key</span>, rail limits and value date</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="rule">Rule</span></li>
<li class="pdd-bd-step" data-m="ok"><div class="pdd-bd-sh"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><circle cx="18" cy="24" r="12"/><circle cx="30" cy="24" r="12"/><path d="M20 24l3 4 6-8"/></svg></span><b>7 Reconcile and evidence</b></div><p><i>Fails</i> No proof of what was approved</p><p><i>Caught by</i> A <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#24">signed record</span> of the approval</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="rule">Rule</span></li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="pdd-bd-p" data-p="int" role="region" aria-label="Integrations">
<p class="pdd-bd-ilead"><b>Three layers.</b> <span class="pdd-bd-ly" data-ly="id">Identity</span><span class="pdd-bd-ly" data-ly="man">Mandate</span><span class="pdd-bd-ly" data-ly="rail">Rail</span><span class="pdd-bd-ly" data-ly="ctl">Controls</span> <span class="pdd-view">Year one: identity, plus ACH and RTP through the bank API.</span></p>
<ul class="pdd-bd-tiles">
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="ctl" data-m="flow"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><rect x="8" y="8" width="32" height="32" rx="4"/><path d="M8 18h32M18 18v22"/></svg></span><b>ERP and AP</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-src="N#31 N#32 N#8" data-note="Oracle (October 2025), Coupa and BILL agents run payments. Coupa: 2,395 payments worth $20.1M in 5 weeks at one customer. Vendor claims.">Live</span> Oracle, Coupa and BILL agents run payments.</p></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="rail" data-m="bank"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M6 18 24 8l18 10z"/><path d="M11 18v18M20 18v18M28 18v18M37 18v18M6 40h36"/></svg></span><b>Bank rails</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-src="N#37 N#4" data-note="RTP limit $10M since February 9, 2025. Nacha fraud monitoring phase 2 took effect June 19, 2026.">Live</span> ACH and RTP through your bank API, year one. RTP up to $10M.</p></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="rail" data-m="bank"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M8 24c5-9 10-13 16-13s11 4 16 13c-5 9-10 13-16 13S13 33 8 24z"/><circle cx="24" cy="24" r="5"/></svg></span><b>Bank data access</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-src="N#10" data-note="JPMorgan allows read-only agent access through Plaid. The report covers consumer accounts. No bank-branded treasury API for agent payments was found.">Live</span> Read only. Large banks do not let agents pay on consumer accounts.</p></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="man" data-m="coin"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><rect x="12" y="6" width="24" height="36" rx="3"/><path d="M18 16h12M18 23h12"/><path d="M18 32l4 4 8-8"/></svg></span><b>Google AP2</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="spec" data-src="N#15 N#14" data-note="AP2 v0.2. Standardization moves to FIDO, where Mastercard and Visa chair the Payments group.">Spec</span> Signed mandates with Verifiable Intent, moving to FIDO.</p></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="man" data-m="coin"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M8 12h6l5 20h18l5-14H16"/><circle cx="21" cy="38" r="3"/><circle cx="35" cy="38" r="3"/></svg></span><b>OpenAI and Stripe ACP</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="spec" data-src="N#16 N#17 M#9" data-note="Latest stable spec dated 2026-04-17, labelled beta, with a Delegate Payment spec. ChatGPT Instant Checkout ended in March 2026.">Spec</span> Beta, with a Delegate Payment flow.</p></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="id" data-m="flow"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><rect x="6" y="12" width="36" height="24" rx="4"/><path d="M6 20h36M12 30h8"/></svg></span><b>Visa Intelligent Commerce</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="pilot" data-src="N#8 N#20 N#9" data-note="Live but controlled for consumers. <li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="id" data-m="risk"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><circle cx="19" cy="24" r="11"/><circle cx="29" cy="24" r="11"/></svg></span><b>Mastercard Agent Pay</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-src="N#18" data-note="Every European issuer enabled, 36 banks live with passkeys. <li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="rail" data-m="coin"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><circle cx="24" cy="24" r="16"/><path d="M29 19c-1-2-3-3-5-3-3 0-5 2-5 4s2 3 5 4 5 2 5 4-2 4-5 4c-2 0-4-1-5-3M24 12v4M24 32v4"/></svg></span><b>x402 and MPP</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-src="N#27 S#2" data-note="x402.org, last 30 days: 75.41M transactions, mostly micropayments. <li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="ctl" data-m="ok"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><rect x="10" y="6" width="28" height="36" rx="3"/><path d="M16 16h16M16 24h16M16 32h8"/></svg></span><b>Ledger and reconciliation</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-src="I#27" data-note="A Fed governor says LLMs are used for reconciliation, September 29, 2026.
