<div class="pdd-bd-p" data-p="plan" role="region" aria-label="Delivery">
<p class="pdd-bd-ptag"><span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span></p>
<ol class="pdd-bd-phases">
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-ph" data-m="risk"><p class="pdd-label">Weeks 0 to 6</p><h3 class="pdd-bd-pn">Guard and mandates</h3><p class="pdd-bd-pl">Outcomes</p><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Payee change guard on every channel</li><li>One signed mandate per payment</li><li>Agents read bank data only</li></ul><p class="pdd-bd-pl">Controls</p><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Limits the agent <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#34">cannot change</span></li><li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#35 N#36">Payee name check</span> where a scheme exists</li><li>A <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#4">Nacha monitoring</span> record</li></ul></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-ph" data-m="rule"><p class="pdd-label">Weeks 6 to 12</p><h3 class="pdd-bd-pn">Agent sandbox</h3><p class="pdd-bd-pl">Outcomes</p><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Agent drafts real payments in shadow</li><li>AI scores checked against people</li><li>One rail, one entity, one bank</li></ul><p class="pdd-bd-pl">Controls</p><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>A <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#12">person releases</span> every new payee</li><li>Agent <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#21" data-note="Web Bot Auth signatures, Cloudflare, October 2025.">signs every request</span></li><li>Agent limits in the <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#6">security procedure</span></li></ul></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-ph" data-m="ok"><p class="pdd-label">Week 12 onward</p><h3 class="pdd-bd-pn">Scaled rails</h3><p class="pdd-bd-pl">Outcomes</p><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Add <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#37">RTP</span> and <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#20 N#18">card agent tokens</span></li><li>Low-risk payments release by rule</li><li>An evidence pack per payment</li></ul><p class="pdd-bd-pl">Controls</p><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>A person releases outside the matrix</li><li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#38">Liability terms</span> per rail</li><li>Quarterly review of mandates</li></ul></li>
</ol>
<div class="pdd-panel pdd-apc" data-m="ok" role="group" aria-labelledby="pdd-apc-h"><div class="pdd-apc-head"><h3 class="pdd-apc-t" id="pdd-apc-h">Your AP desk</h3><span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span><p class="pdd-apc-say">Our arithmetic. Cross-border always goes to a person.</p></div><ul class="pdd-apc-out" aria-live="polite"><li data-m="ok"><b class="pdd-num" data-apc="save">$195K</b><span>yearly saving</span></li><li data-m="flow"><b class="pdd-num" data-apc="back">25</b><span>payback month</span></li><li data-m="rule"><b class="pdd-num" data-apc="hrs">15.8</b><span>checker hours a day</span></li><li data-m="bank"><b class="pdd-num" data-apc="stp">45%</b><span>straight through</span></li></ul><div class="pdd-apc-mix" role="img" aria-label="Payment mix: 65% ACH, 10% card, 25% cross-border. 45% released by rule."><span class="pdd-apc-seg" data-m="bank" data-seg="ach" style="--w:65%"></span><span class="pdd-apc-seg" data-m="flow" data-seg="card" style="--w:10%"></span><span class="pdd-apc-seg" data-m="coin" data-seg="cross" style="--w:25%"></span><i class="pdd-apc-stp" style="--w:45%"></i></div><p class="pdd-apc-key"><span data-m="bank"><i class="pdd-dot"></i>ACH <b data-apc="ach">65%</b></span><span data-m="flow"><i class="pdd-dot"></i>Card <b data-apc="card">10%</b></span><span data-m="coin"><i class="pdd-dot"></i>Cross-border <b data-apc="cross">25%</b></span><span class="pdd-apc-kl">Line: released by rule</span></p><details class="pdd-more pdd-apc-in"><summary>Your inputs</summary><p class="pdd-apc-how">Illustration values, except cost per payment at the <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="I#18" data-note="WEX restates a 2025 Ardent Partners benchmark: $9.40 to process an average invoice, $2.78 best in class. Applied per payment as an illustration.">WEX average</span>. Saving counts that cost on payments released by rule, less run cost and fraud loss. 250 working days.</p><div class="pdd-apc-grid"><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="cost"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-sup">Suppliers</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-sup">3,000</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-sup" data-k="sup" min="500" max="20000" step="100" value="3000"></div><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="cost"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-pay">Payments a year</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-pay">72,000</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-pay" data-k="pay" min="6000" max="600000" step="1000" value="72000"></div><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="bank"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-ach">ACH share</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-ach">65%</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-ach" data-k="ach" min="0" max="100" step="5" value="65"></div><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="flow"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-card">Card share</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-card">10%</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-card" data-k="card" min="0" max="100" step="5" value="10"></div><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="cost"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-cost">Cost per payment</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-cost">$9.40</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-cost" data-k="cost" min="2.78" max="10.89" step="0.01" value="9.4"></div><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="ok"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-rule">Released by rule</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-rule">60%</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-rule" data-k="rule" min="0" max="100" step="5" value="60"></div><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="rule"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-min">Minutes per hold</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-min">6</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-min" data-k="min" min="1" max="30" step="1" value="6"></div><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="rule"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-build">Build cost</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-build">$400K</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-build" data-k="build" min="50000" max="2000000" step="25000" value="400000"></div><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="rule"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-run">AWS run a year, our arithmetic</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-run">$15K</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-run" data-k="run" min="5000" max="500000" step="5000" value="15000"></div><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="risk"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-loss">Fraud loss a year</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-loss">$50K</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-loss" data-k="loss" min="0" max="500000" step="5000" value="50000"></div></div></details></div>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-bd-qs"><summary>Questions to ask your bank</summary><ol class="pdd-bd-ql"><li>Will you let our agent start a payment, or <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#10">read only</span>?</li><li>How do agent limits enter our <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#6">security procedure agreement</span>?</li><li>Do you issue <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#19 N#18">agent tokens</span> on Visa or Mastercard?</li><li>Who carries the loss when an agent <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#10 N#11" data-note="BofA is working out who is liable when an AI authorizes a payment. JPMorgan's CFO said customers who share credentials with agents could be responsible.">pays the wrong supplier</span>?</li><li>How does your <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#5">Nacha fraud monitoring</span> treat our agent's payments?</li><li>As an Originator, how do we meet our own <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#5">monitoring duty</span>?</li><li>Does our insurance cover agent-initiated transfers? Ask the insurer too.</li></ol></details>
</div>
<div class="pdd-bd-p" data-p="verdict" role="region" aria-label="Verdict">

<article class="pdd-panel pdd-vd-call" data-m="ok"><div class="pdd-vd-ch"><svg class="pdd-vd-ico" viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M24 7v33M15 41h18M9 13h30"/><path d="M9 13 4 25a5 5 0 0 0 10 0zM39 13l-5 12a5 5 0 0 0 10 0z"/></svg><div><p class="pdd-label">The call</p><h3 class="pdd-vd-h pdd-view">Year one: a signed agent identity, plus ACH and RTP through your bank API.</h3><p class="pdd-vd-sub">The card lane is pilot-ready. A release matrix decides who releases.</p></div></div><ul class="pdd-vd-fix"><li data-m="risk">A rule releases known payees in band. A person releases the rest</li><li data-m="rule">No LLM on approval path. ML score can only add a hold.</li><li data-m="coin">Stablecoin lane 9b stays off until its policy gate clears</li></ul><div class="pdd-rm pdd-vd-rm"><p class="pdd-rm-h"><b>Release matrix</b> <span class="pdd-tag" data-k="illus">Illustration</span> <span class="pdd-view">Bands are your own limits. RTP and FedNow use a lower band. An ML score can move a cell to Person, never back.</span></p><div class="pdd-rm-sc" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Release matrix, scroll sideways"><table class="pdd-rm-t"><thead><tr><th data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div><div class="pdd-vd-drs"><details class="pdd-more pdd-vd-dr"><summary>Why this protocol stack</summary><ol class="pdd-ps"><li data-ly="id"><b>Identity</b><span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#21 N#22" data-note="Web Bot Auth: HTTP Message Signatures (RFC 9421) with a nonce. Visa Trusted Agent Protocol, Mastercard Agent Pay and Amex use it.">Web Bot Auth</span> over RFC 9421. Visa TAP, Mastercard Agent Pay and Amex use it.</span></li><li data-ly="man"><b>Mandate</b><span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#15" data-note="AP2 v0.2 signs mandates and is moving to FIDO.">AP2</span> and <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#24">Verifiable Intent</span>, via FIDO.</span></li><li data-ly="rail"><b>Rail</b><span>Bank API for ACH and RTP, card token, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="S#2" data-note="MPP, maintained by Tempo and Stripe, has methods for Tempo, Stripe and ACH.">MPP</span> with an ACH method, or x402.</span></li></ol><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li><b>Signs:</b> your agent platform, with a key in the Visa Agentic Directory, as <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#8" data-note="BILL&#39;s Payment Agent is registered through Trusted Agent Protocol for the Visa pilot of October 20, 2026.">BILL did</span>. <b>Verifies:</b> the supplier portal or your bank edge.</li><li>Agent Pay is live for <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#18">European consumers</span> only. The Visa B2B pilot has <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#9">no volumes</span> yet.</li><li>AWS WAF can <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#18">pre-check</span> signatures at the edge. ACP is <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#16">still in beta</span>.</li></ul></details><details class="pdd-more pdd-vd-dr"><summary>Why the guard stays</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Under <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#6">UCC 4A-202</span> the payer is bound when the bank followed the agreed procedure.</li><li>Baltimore approved a <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="K#1">changed vendor account</span> without checking it.</li><li>Nacha now requires monitoring for <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#5">false pretenses</span>.</li><li>AgentCore Policy can require an <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#15">approval before a transfer</span>.</li><li>Stripe agent tools need a person to <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#7" data-note="Stripe MCP docs: human confirmation before certain agent write actions such as refunds and outbound payments. From December 2026 it stops accepting full-access keys and restricted keys without the Agent tag.">confirm refunds and payouts</span>. Plain keys stop working in December 2026.</li></ul></details><details class="pdd-more pdd-vd-dr"><summary>What would change the call</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Visa or Mastercard publish agent liability rules that give mandates weight. Move the card lane from pilot to primary. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#4 N#9" data-note="Dispute rules say little about agents today, and the Visa B2B pilot sets no liability framework.">Evidence</span></li><li><span class="pdd-view">The dispute pilot shows mandates carry no weight. Switch to a protocol hub.</span></li><li>The Visa, BILL and Billtrust pilot shows volumes or reaches general release. Put the supplier portal path first. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#9">Pilot</span></li><li>Big banks let outside agents move money. Add a bank API agent channel. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#10">Today</span></li><li>All four policy gates clear: permitted issuer, Treasury AML and sanctions rule, a corridor price win and payee consent. Switch on lane 9b. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2 O#31">Gates</span></li><li>UK HM Treasury or the EU PSR assigns liability for agent payments. Rerun the loss table. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#6 O#5" data-note="HM Treasury asked in July 2026 whether liability rules need updating for agentic payments. The EU PSR deal is provisional.">Consultations</span></li><li>ACP ships a stable release with a major B2B adopter. Add it at the mandate layer. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#16">Beta today</span></li></ul></details><details class="pdd-more pdd-vd-dr"><summary>Runner-up and caveats</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>JPMorganChase, runner-up: <span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="spec" data-src="N#12" data-note="JPMorganChase plans maker-checker controls for agents in treasury, October 2026.">Plan</span> agent controls, a <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#12" data-note="J.P. Morgan Payments and Mirakl signed a global agreement on March 10, 2026 for agentic commerce payments, tokens and fraud protection.">Mirakl deal</span>, and it <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#10">blocks outside agents</span>.</li><li>Skyfire, to watch: <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#25">KYAPay</span> is an individual IETF draft.</li><li>Six banks, Bank of America and NatWest among them, set <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#4" data-note="Bank of America, September 22, 2026: ASB, Bank of America, Capital One, Commonwealth Bank of Australia, ING and NatWest published joint principles for trusted agentic commerce. No liability allocation.">agent commerce principles</span> on September 22, 2026. None assigns liability yet.</li><li>Ramp's Visa agents have no launch date. Natural names no customers. Rain gives no date or volumes.</li></ul></details></div></article>
<article class="pdd-vd-v pdd-panel pdd-vd-path" data-v="path" data-m="ok"><div class="pdd-vd-tt"><div class="pdd-vd-tc" data-m="risk"><p class="pdd-vd-th">Today</p><ul><li>Payee changes checked by phone call-back</li><li>Approvals keyed into a bank portal</li><li>Batch ACH and wire, with cut-offs</li></ul><p class="pdd-vd-tf"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#7" data-note="AFP 2026 survey: 74% of firms were hit by email compromise in 2025.">74%</span> of firms hit by email compromise in 2025</p></div><div class="pdd-vd-tc" data-m="ok"><p class="pdd-vd-th">Target 2027</p><ul><li><span class="pdd-view">An agent proposes. The release matrix picks a rule or a person.</span></li><li>ACH and RTP first, card tokens next</li><li>Stablecoin lane 9b once its policy gate clears</li></ul><p class="pdd-vd-tf"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#8" data-note="Ledger Insights, October 1, 2026: Open USD went live about September 30, 2026, with Coinbase, Mastercard, Shopify, Stripe and Visa as founding investors. A secondary report.">Open USD</span> live since September 30, 2026</p></div><div class="pdd-vd-tc pdd-vd-chg" data-m="flow"><p class="pdd-vd-th">What changes</p><dl><dt>Who acts</dt><dd><span class="pdd-vd-was">A clerk keys it</span><span class="pdd-vd-now">The agent proposes</span></dd><dt>Who checks</dt><dd><span class="pdd-vd-was">Sees the amount</span><span class="pdd-vd-now">Sees the payee source</span></dd><dt>Rail</dt><dd><span class="pdd-vd-was">Batch ACH or wire</span><span class="pdd-vd-now">Several rails, 24/7</span></dd><dt>Evidence</dt><dd><span class="pdd-vd-was">Inboxes, spreadsheets</span><span class="pdd-vd-now">A write-once record</span></dd></dl></div></div><div class="pdd-vd-pr"><ol class="pdd-vd-p3"><li data-m="risk"><span class="pdd-vd-jn">1</span><span><b>Now</b> Identity layer, payee guard, ACH and RTP</span></li><li data-m="coin"><span class="pdd-vd-jn">2</span><span><b>From <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2 O#31">January 18, 2027</span></b> Lane 9b pilot on one corridor, if all four gates clear</span></li><li data-m="ok"><span class="pdd-vd-jn">3</span><span><b>Scale</b> Card lane. Rules release more known payees</span></li></ol><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-vd-go" data-go="target">See the target design</button></div></article>

<article class="pdd-panel pdd-vd-col" data-m="bank"><p class="pdd-vd-hd"><svg class="pdd-ar-i" viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><rect x="10" y="8" width="20" height="34" rx="2"/><path d="M30 18h8v24M15 15h4M22 15h3M15 22h4M22 22h3M15 29h4M22 29h3M6 42h36"/></svg><b>Enterprise</b></p><ol class="pdd-vd-ps"><li class="pdd-vd-p"><span class="pdd-vd-rk">1</span><div><p class="pdd-vd-pn"><b>Visa</b><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="pilot" data-src="N#8" data-note="Visa, BILL and Billtrust, October 20, 2026: AI agents pay approved buyer invoices end to end through supplier portals. BILL's Payment Agent is registered through Trusted Agent Protocol.">Pilot</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-pw">Agents pay invoices in supplier portals, plus one protocol <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#24" data-note="Visa Intelligent Commerce Connect, April 8, 2026: one on-ramp for Trusted Agent Protocol, MPP, ACP and UCP. In pilot with partners including AWS.">on-ramp</span>.</p></div></li><li class="pdd-vd-p"><span class="pdd-vd-rk">2</span><div><p class="pdd-vd-pn"><b>Mastercard</b><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-src="N#18" data-note="June 2, 2026: every Mastercard issuer in Europe is enabled for Agent Pay at network level, and 36 banks completed controlled live payments with passkeys.">Live</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-pw">Agent Pay on every European issuer, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#18">36 banks</span> live. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#24">Verifiable Intent</span> is open.</p></div></li><li class="pdd-vd-p"><span class="pdd-vd-rk">3</span><div><p class="pdd-vd-pn"><b>Stripe</b><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-src="H#21" data-note="Stripe Sessions, April 29, 2026: MPP agent payments accepted in stablecoins and in fiat. Issuing for agents was a preview.">Live</span><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="spec" data-src="H#21" data-note="Issuing for agents, single-use virtual cards, was previewed at Sessions on April 29, 2026.">Preview</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-pw"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#1" data-note="Stripe Shared Payment Tokens carry limits for currency, maximum amount and expiry, and the agent can revoke them.">Tokens</span> capped by amount and expiry. MPP in stablecoin and fiat.</p></div></li></ol></article>
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-vd-col" data-m="flow"><p class="pdd-vd-hd"><svg class="pdd-ar-i" viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M24 5c7 5 10 12 10 20l-3 8H17l-3-8c0-8 3-15 10-20z"/><circle cx="24" cy="19" r="3"/><path d="M17 33l-4 9 7-3M31 33l4 9-7-3"/></svg><b>Startups</b></p><ol class="pdd-vd-ps"><li class="pdd-vd-p"><span class="pdd-vd-rk">1</span><div><p class="pdd-vd-pn"><b>Ramp</b><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-src="O#26" data-note="Ramp Agents for AP, launched October 7, 2025: code invoices, recommend approvals, pay by card in vendor portals and check for fraud. A vendor claim.">Live</span><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="pilot" data-src="O#25" data-note="Reported April 2, 2026: Ramp plans AI agents for corporate bill pay on Visa Intelligent Commerce and Trusted Agent Protocol. No launch date.">Planned</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-pw">AP agents code, approve and pay invoices. Visa agents planned.</p></div></li><li class="pdd-vd-p"><span class="pdd-vd-rk">2</span><div><p class="pdd-vd-pn"><b>Natural</b><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-src="O#27" data-note="July 21, 2026: six products generally available, Wallets (FDIC-insured accounts for agents), Vaults, Pay, Request, Transfer and Connect. Raised a $30M Series A. Names no customers.">Live</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-pw"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#27">Six products</span> live, with FDIC-insured agent wallets. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#27">$30M</span> raised.</p></div></li><li class="pdd-vd-p"><span class="pdd-vd-rk">3</span><div><p class="pdd-vd-pn"><b>Rain</b><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-src="O#28" data-note="Rain says agents have used its scoped virtual cards in production for months. The Agent Control Layer is in beta. The page has no date or volumes.">Live</span><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="spec" data-src="O#28" data-note="Agent Control Layer, beta: agent and program limits enforced at card issue and at transfer start.">Beta</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-pw">Stablecoin-funded scoped cards. An alliance of <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#29 R#3" data-note="Rain launched the Agentic Payments Alliance on August 18, 2026 with 26 founding members, including Visa, Mastercard, Fiserv and Circle.">26 founders</span>, Visa and Mastercard among them.</p></div></li></ol></article>
<p class="pdd-vd-pw pdd-vd-open"><b>Open lanes</b> <span class="pdd-view">US payee validation, a cross-network mandate evidence pack, mid-market ERP connectors and supplier-side agent verification.</span></p>
</div>
<article class="pdd-vd-v pdd-panel pdd-vd-coin" data-v="coin" data-m="coin"><div class="pdd-vd-cl"><p class="pdd-vd-hd"><svg class="pdd-ar-i" viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><circle cx="24" cy="24" r="16"/><path d="M29 19c-1-2-3-3-5-3-3 0-5 2-5 4s2 3 5 4 5 2 5 4-2 4-5 4c-2 0-4-1-5-3M24 12v4M24 32v4"/></svg><b>Stablecoin rail</b></p><p class="pdd-vd-pw"><span class="pdd-view">Optional, behind the rail picker.</span> The same payee guard plus wallet screening. Off until all four gates clear.</p><ol class="pdd-vd-gates"><li><span class="pdd-vd-jn">1</span><span>A coin from a permitted issuer. The <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2 O#31" data-note="The OCC final rule for payment stablecoin issuers takes effect on January 18, 2027. It binds issuers, not payers.">OCC rule</span> binds issuers, not payers</span></li><li><span class="pdd-vd-jn">2</span><span>The <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2" data-note="AML and sanctions requirements come in a separate Treasury rulemaking, still to come.">Treasury AML and sanctions rule</span></span></li><li><span class="pdd-vd-jn">3</span><span>A corridor price win. One test found <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#21" data-note="Banca d&#39;Italia sent 200 USDC across ten corridors in March 2026 and found no systematic advantage over traditional channels.">no systematic edge</span></span></li><li><span class="pdd-vd-jn">4</span><span>Payee consent</span></li></ol></div><div class="pdd-vd-cr"><div class="pdd-vd-wl"><div><p class="pdd-vd-wh" data-k="win">Wins</p><ul><li>Cross-border payouts, weekends included, to suppliers who take USDC</li><li>Agent payments under <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#14" data-note="AWS docs describe AgentCore payments for agent microtransactions, often under $1, with a budget and an expiry per session.">$1</span></li><li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#6" data-note="The Block, June 10, 2026: a layer on the Mastercard network across cards, accounts and stablecoins, with spending limits enforced in code.">Mastercard Agent Pay for Machines</span> runs on cards, accounts and stablecoins</li><li>Spend limits written into the payment <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#13 O#28" data-note="AgentCore payments enforces payment limits at the infrastructure layer. Rain enforces limits at card issue and transfer start.">itself</span></li></ul></div><div><p class="pdd-vd-wh" data-k="lose">Does not win</p><ul><li>Domestic dollars: RTP and FedNow run 24/7, up to <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#37 O#33">$10M</span></li><li>High value: Fedwire averages <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#10" data-note="Fedwire Funds moved about $1.148 quadrillion across 217.3 million transfers in 2025.">$5.28M</span></li><li>Thin demand: the market sits near <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#1" data-note="FSB, October 12, 2026: stablecoin market value around $300 billion, essentially unchanged from a year earlier.">$300B</span></li><li>Suppliers who want fiat. Four big US banks plan a <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#9" data-note="The Paypers citing the WSJ, June 5, 2026: JPMorgan Chase, Bank of America, Citigroup and Wells Fargo plan a shared tokenized deposit network for the first half of 2027.">24/7 tokenized deposit network</span> for 2027</li></ul></div></div><details class="pdd-more pdd-vd-dr"><summary>Read the volumes with care</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Visa's <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#30" data-note="Visa, September 8, 2026: stablecoin settlement passed a $20B annualized run rate. That is card settlement.">$20B run rate</span> is card settlement, not supplier payouts.</li><li>x402 moved <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#27">$24.24M</span> in 30 days, and about 4,000 wallets make up <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#2" data-note="Reported April 21, 2026: about 4,000 wallets drive roughly 90% of adjusted x402 spending.">roughly 90%</span> of spend.</li><li>ACH carried <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="L#2" data-note="Nacha: ACH payments in 2025 were worth $93 trillion, with close to 8.1 billion B2B payments.">$93T</span> in 2025, including 8.1B B2B payments.</li></ul></details></div></article>
<article class="pdd-vd-v pdd-panel pdd-vd-jobs" data-v="jobs" data-m="ai"><p class="pdd-vd-hd"><svg class="pdd-ar-i" viewBox="0 0 48 48" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><rect x="11" y="16" width="26" height="22" rx="6"/><path d="M24 16V10"/><circle cx="24" cy="7.5" r="2.5"/><circle cx="19" cy="26" r="2.5"/><circle cx="29" cy="26" r="2.5"/><path d="M19 33h10"/></svg><b>Six jobs for the payment agent</b></p><ol class="pdd-vd-js"><li class="pdd-vd-j"><p class="pdd-vd-jh"><span class="pdd-vd-jn">1</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#15 O#1" data-note="AP2 signs mandates. Stripe tokens carry amount, currency and expiry limits.">Intent capture</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-jw">Drafts the mandate: who, cap, expiry</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="ai">Agent drafts, person signs</span></li><li class="pdd-vd-j"><p class="pdd-vd-jh"><span class="pdd-vd-jn">2</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#31 O#26" data-note="Oracle and Ramp agents match and code invoices. Vendor claims.">Invoice match</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-jw">Codes the invoice and runs the match</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="ai">Agent does</span></li><li class="pdd-vd-j"><p class="pdd-vd-jh"><span class="pdd-vd-jn">3</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#5 O#26 N#35 N#36" data-note="Nacha false-pretenses monitoring, Ramp fraud checks, payee name checks in the EU and UK.">Payee risk flag</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-jw">New payee, changed bank details, name mismatch</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="person">Agent flags, release held</span></li><li class="pdd-vd-j"><p class="pdd-vd-jh"><span class="pdd-vd-jn">4</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#37 O#33" data-note="RTP and FedNow limits are $10M per payment.">Rail choice</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-jw">Limits, cut-offs, cost and value date</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="rule">Rule decides</span></li><li class="pdd-vd-j"><p class="pdd-vd-jh"><span class="pdd-vd-jn">5</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#32 N#31" data-note="Coupa batch agents and Oracle payment tracking. Vendor claims.">Exceptions</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-jw">Batches, matches and chases</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="ai">Agent does, person clears</span></li><li class="pdd-vd-j"><p class="pdd-vd-jh"><span class="pdd-vd-jn">6</span><span>Pay date</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-jw">Due date against an early-pay discount</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="rule">Agent proposes, rule checks</span></li></ol><p class="pdd-vd-pw pdd-vd-jf">The release matrix sends a new or changed payee, or a high band, to a <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#12 O#15 O#23" data-note="Maker-checker controls at JPMorganChase. AgentCore Policy can require an approval. Step Functions can wait for the checker.">checker</span>.</p></article>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</section>
<section class="pdd-scene pdd-s2-scene" id="pdd-path" data-rail="bank">
<p class="pdd-kicker"><b>4</b>The money path</p>
<h2 class="pdd-q">What does a payout system need, and who loses if it breaks?</h2>
<p class="pdd-a">Ten states and thirteen parts carry every payout. <span class="pdd-view">Only the rail changes.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: who holds the money, and who loses.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: the states, the faults and the resend rule.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">For you: where the stablecoin leg differs from the bank.</span></p>
<div class="pdd-stage">
<div class="pdd-s2-bar"><div class="pdd-seg pdd-s2-view" data-key="view" role="group" aria-label="View"><button type="button" data-val="states" aria-pressed="true">States</button><button type="button" data-val="comp" aria-pressed="false">Components</button></div><div class="pdd-seg pdd-s2-rail" data-key="rail" role="group" aria-label="Rail for this payout"><button type="button" data-val="bank" data-m="bank" aria-pressed="true">Bank rail</button><button type="button" data-val="coin" data-m="coin" aria-pressed="false">Stablecoin rail</button></div><p class="pdd-s2-off"><span class="pdd-dot" data-m="coin"></span>Stablecoin route is off by default. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#21 E#13" data-note="200 USDC transfers at small ticket size. Both rows describe the same Banca d'Italia study. No like-for-like match with B2B payouts.">One test cost 0.30% to 8.96%</span></p><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-run"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 2.5v11l9-5.5z"/></svg>Run a payout</button></div>
<div class="pdd-grid">
<div class="pdd-c8 pdd-s2-main">
<div class="pdd-s2-faults" data-m="risk" role="group" aria-label="Ten faults"><span class="pdd-label"><span class="pdd-dot"></span>Break it</span><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="sanctions" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Sanctions match at re-screen, state 7">Sanctions match</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="wrong" aria-pressed="true" aria-label="Sent to wrong payee, state 8">Wrong payee</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="ach" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="ACH returned, state 8">ACH returned</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="unknown" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Unknown outcome, state 7">Unknown outcome</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="bankexit" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Partner bank exits, state 7">Bank exits</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="depeg" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Token off its peg, state 6">Token off peg</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="chain" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Chain halted, state 7">Chain halted</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="wallet" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Platform wallet frozen, state 7">Wallet frozen</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="frozen" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Payee address frozen, state 8">Address frozen</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="offramp" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Off-ramp fails after deposit, state 8">Off-ramp fails</button></div>
<div class="pdd-s2-trackw" style="--p:7" data-end="fault"><ol class="pdd-s2-track" aria-label="Ten payout states"><li><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="0" data-done aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 1, Drafted"><b>1</b><span>Drafted</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="flow"></i></li><li><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="1" data-done aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 2, Verified"><b>2</b><span>Verified</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="flow"></i></li><li><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="2" data-done aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 3, Approved"><b>3</b><span>Approved</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="flow"></i></li><li data-end="all"><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="3" data-done aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 4, Screened"><b>4</b><span>Screened</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="flow"></i></li><li><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="4" data-done aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 5, Funded"><b>5</b><span>Funded</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="bank"></i></li><li data-end="coin"><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="5" data-done aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 6, Routed"><b>6</b><span>Routed</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="bank"></i></li><li data-end="all" data-hd><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="6" data-diff data-done aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 7, Submitted"><b>7</b><span>Submitted</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="bank"></i></li><li data-end="all" data-hd><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="7" data-diff data-done aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 8, Settled"><b>8</b><span>Settled</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="bank"></i></li><li><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="8" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 9, Delivered"><b>9</b><span>Delivered</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="ok"></i></li><li><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="9" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 10, Reconciled"><b>10</b><span>Reconciled</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="ok"></i></li></ol><span class="pdd-s2-token" aria-hidden="true"></span></div>
<p class="pdd-s2-key"><span class="pdd-cap"><span class="pdd-dot pdd-s2-rdot"></span>States 7 to 8 change with the rail</span><span class="pdd-label">Who holds the money</span></p>
<ol class="pdd-s2-holders" aria-label="Holder of the funds at each state"><li data-m="flow" data-bank="0-3" data-coin="0-3"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="14" y="18" width="34" height="48" rx="4"/><path d="M24 30h14M24 40h14M24 50h14"/><path d="M54 42h14M62 35l7 7-7 7"/></svg><b>Payer</b><span class="pdd-label">States 1 to 4</span></li><li data-m="bank" data-bank="4-6" data-coin="4-5"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="12" y="16" width="56" height="46" rx="6"/><circle cx="40" cy="39" r="11"/><path d="M40 28v6M40 44v6M29 39h6M45 39h6M20 62v6M60 62v6"/></svg><b>For-benefit account</b><span class="pdd-label" data-r="bank">States 5 to 7</span><span class="pdd-label" data-r="coin">States 5 to 6</span></li><li data-m="bank" data-bank="7-7" class="is-on"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M10 32l30-18 30 18z"/><path d="M18 38v22M32 38v22M48 38v22M62 38v22M10 66h60"/></svg><b>Receiving bank</b><span class="pdd-label">State 8</span></li><li data-m="coin" data-coin="6-6"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="10" y="22" width="60" height="40" rx="6"/><path d="M10 34h60"/><circle cx="30" cy="48" r="5"/><path d="M35 48h17M46 48v5M52 48v4"/></svg><b>Custodian wallet</b><span class="pdd-label">State 7</span></li><li data-m="coin" data-coin="7-7"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 28a6 6 0 0 1 6-6h36v40H18a6 6 0 0 1-6-6z"/><path d="M12 32h42"/><path d="M48 44h22M63 37l7 7-7 7"/></svg><b>Off-ramp or payee wallet</b><span class="pdd-label">State 8</span></li><li data-m="ok" data-bank="8-9" data-coin="8-9"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="34" cy="26" r="11"/><path d="M12 66c2-14 11-20 22-20 6 0 11 2 15 6"/><path d="M50 58l7 7 13-15"/></svg><b>Payee</b><span class="pdd-label">States 9 to 10</span></li></ol>
</div>
<aside class="pdd-drawer pdd-c4 pdd-s2-drawer" data-open="true" data-m="risk" aria-live="polite" aria-label="Detail for the selected state or fault"><p class="pdd-label pdd-s2-dk"><span class="pdd-dot"></span><span class="pdd-s2-dkt">Fault at state 8</span></p><h3 class="pdd-s2-dt">Sent to wrong payee</h3><div class="pdd-s2-sec"><p class="pdd-label">What happens</p><p class="pdd-s2-what">One provider&#39;s RTP terms say a receiving bank need not return funds on request.</p></div><div class="pdd-s2-sec"><p class="pdd-label"><span class="pdd-s2-h2">Who bears the loss</span></p><p class="pdd-s2-two">No bearer is stated on the bank rail.</p></div><div class="pdd-s2-sec pdd-s2-rs"><p class="pdd-label pdd-s2-h3">Rule that prevents it</p><p class="pdd-s2-rule">Payee and method are verified at state 2, before approval.</p></div><p class="pdd-s2-chips"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="F#16" data-note="The Clearing House describes its own network. Undated page.">RTP is final and irrevocable</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="G#33" data-note="A payment provider's RTP terms, last updated February 5, 2024.">Need not return funds</span></p></aside>
</div>
<div class="pdd-s2-map" role="group" aria-label="Payout architecture. Thirteen components in four bands share one instruction model and one ledger, and only the rail adapters differ between bank and stablecoin.">
<div class="pdd-s2-band" data-m="flow">
<p class="pdd-label pdd-s2-bandh"><span class="pdd-dot"></span>Workflow</p>
<div class="pdd-s2-nodes"><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C3" data-m="flow"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="16" y="14" width="48" height="56" rx="6"/><path d="M30 14v-5h20v5"/><path d="M25 34l5 5 9-10M25 53l5 5 9-10M46 36h10M46 55h10"/></svg><span><i>C3</i>Approvals and instruction</span></div><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C1" data-m="flow"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="32" cy="26" r="11"/><path d="M12 64c0-13 9-20 20-20s20 7 20 20"/><path d="M52 32l6 6 12-14"/></svg><span><i>C1</i>Payee onboarding</span></div><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C2" data-m="flow"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="18" y="10" width="44" height="60" rx="6"/><circle cx="31" cy="31" r="5"/><circle cx="49" cy="49" r="5"/><path d="M51 29L29 51"/></svg><span><i>C2</i>Tax and title</span></div></div>
</div>
<div class="pdd-s2-band" data-m="rule" data-stag="1">
<p class="pdd-label pdd-s2-bandh"><span class="pdd-dot"></span>Control</p>
<div class="pdd-s2-nodes"><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C4" data-m="rule"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M40 8l26 10v20c0 16-11 28-26 34-15-6-26-18-26-34V18z"/><circle cx="38" cy="36" r="9"/><path d="M45 43l9 9"/></svg><span><i>C4</i>Compliance screening</span></div><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C5" data-m="rule"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="13" cy="40" r="5"/><path d="M18 40h14"/><path d="M32 28v24"/><path d="M32 40c12 0 10-20 24-20h10M32 40c12 0 10 20 24 20h10"/><path d="M60 14l6 6-6 6M60 54l6 6-6 6"/></svg><span><i>C5</i>Router and gates</span></div><a class="pdd-s2-node is-ai" href="#pdd-ai" data-c="C12" data-m="rule"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M32 10l5 15 15 5-15 5-5 15-5-15-15-5 15-5z"/><path d="M46 56c5-8 19-8 24 0-5 8-19 8-24 0z"/><circle cx="58" cy="56" r="3"/></svg><span><i>C12</i>AI assist</span></a><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C13" data-m="rule"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M10 56a30 30 0 0 1 60 0z"/><path d="M40 56l15-18"/><path d="M40 26v6M20 40l5 3M60 40l-2 1"/><path d="M26 66h28"/></svg><span><i>C13</i>Counterparty risk</span></div></div>
</div>
<div class="pdd-s2-band">
<p class="pdd-label pdd-s2-bandh"><span class="pdd-dot" data-m="bank"></span><span class="pdd-dot" data-m="coin"></span>Rails</p>
<div class="pdd-s2-nodes"><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C6" data-m="bank"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M10 30l30-18 30 18z"/><path d="M19 36v24M33 36v24M47 36v24M61 36v24"/><path d="M10 67h60"/></svg><span><i>C6</i>Bank rail adapters</span></div><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C7" data-m="coin"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="40" cy="40" r="26"/><path d="M49 32c-2-5-16-6-17 2s16 5 17 13-14 7-18 1"/><path d="M40 20v40"/></svg><span><i>C7</i>Stablecoin adapter</span></div><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C8" data-m="coin"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="25" cy="40" r="13"/><circle cx="25" cy="40" r="3"/><path d="M38 40h32M58 40v11M68 40v8"/></svg><span><i>C8</i>Custody and signing</span></div></div>
</div>
<div class="pdd-s2-band" data-m="ok">
<p class="pdd-label pdd-s2-bandh"><span class="pdd-dot"></span>Record</p>
<div class="pdd-s2-nodes"><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C9" data-m="ok"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="12" y="14" width="56" height="52" rx="6"/><path d="M40 14v52"/><path d="M20 28h12M20 40h12M20 52h12M48 28h12M48 40h12M48 52h12"/></svg><span><i>C9</i>Double-entry ledger</span></div><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C10" data-m="ok"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M16 34a25 25 0 0 1 45-10M64 46a25 25 0 0 1-45 10"/><path d="M62 11v13H49M18 69V56h13"/><path d="M30 40l7 7 14-14"/></svg><span><i>C10</i>Reconciliation and audit</span></div><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C11" data-m="ok"><svg viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="12" y="14" width="56" height="48" rx="6"/><circle cx="40" cy="38" r="12"/><path d="M40 26v6M40 44v6M28 38h6M46 38h6M20 62v7M60 62v7"/></svg><span><i>C11</i>Treasury and liquidity</span></div></div>
</div>
</div>

loss</span></td><td></td><td></td><td></td></tr><tr><th scope="row"><button type="button" data-loss="wallet" aria-pressed="false">Platform wallet frozen</button></th><td></td><td></td><td><i transit</button></th><td></td><td></td><td><i class="pdd-s2-mk" data-k="bear"></i><span class="pdd-s2-vh">Bears the loss</span></td><td></td><td></td></tr><tr><th scope="row"><button type="button" data-loss="offramp" aria-pressed="false">Off-ramp fails after deposit</button></th><td></td><td></td><td><i class="pdd-s2-mk" data-k="bear"></i><span class="pdd-s2-vh">Bears the loss</span></td><td></td><td><i class="pdd-s2-mk" data-k="open"></i><span class="pdd-s2-vh">Undecided</span></td></tr></tbody></table><table class="pdd-s2-loss pdd-s2-loss2"><caption <p class="pdd-s2-sr">Bank rail shown.</p>
</div>
<p class="pdd-say">An unknown outcome is resent only after the rail or partner confirms the payment is absent, with the same key. A time limit only escalates to a person.</p>
<script type="application/json" data-pdd="s2-path">{"states":[{"n":"Drafted","d":"The platform keeps its own key. Partner keys may expire after 24 hours.","h":"Payer","ev":[{"t":"First response stored and replayed","src":"D#29","note":"Stripe describes its own API pattern for safe retries. Stripe may prune keys after 24 hours."}]},{"n":"Verified","d":"Payee and method are current.","h":"Payer"},{"n":"Approved","d":"Amount and payee are bound to the approval.","h":"Payer"},{"n":"Screened","d":"Parties and address are cleared.","h":"Payer","ev":[{"t":"Strict liability","src":"G#17","note":"OFAC 2021: generally strict liability"}]},{"n":"Funded","d":"Irreversible funds are held.","h":"For-benefit account at the partner bank","r":"A final rail is released only against funds that cannot reverse, or a stated platform credit limit.","ev":[{"t":"An ACH debit can come back","src":"D#16","note":"Nacha: an unauthorized consumer debit can be returned for 60 days. Both rows describe the same Banca d'Italia study. No like-for-like match with B2B payouts."}]},{"n":"Submitted","diff":1,"d":{"bank":"Screened again. The rail reference is stored.","coin":"Screened again. Frozen tokens may be unrecoverable.","bear":"The payee is still owed and the platform bears the loss.","j":1,"ev":[{"t":"Issuers must be able to freeze and burn","src":"G#41","note":"Senate Banking Committee fact sheet on the GENIUS Act."},{"t":"Circle may block addresses","src":"F#35","note":"USDC terms dated December 12, 2025."}],"loss":["wallet"]},"frozen":{"n":"Payee address frozen","at":8,"rail":"coin","what":"The payout stops and goes to compliance. <section class="pdd-scene pdd-s3-scene" id="pdd-chooser" aria-label="Rail chooser">
<p class="pdd-kicker"><b>5</b>The rail</p>
<h2 class="pdd-q">Which rail fits this payout?</h2>
<p class="pdd-a">Eight gates run on every payout, and a bank rail is the answer in most cases shown.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: the cheapest safe rail for one payout.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: the eight gates and the rail logic.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">For you: when stablecoin wins, and what it costs.</span></p>
<div class="pdd-stage">

<p class="pdd-s3-pcap" aria-live="polite"><b class="pdd-s3-pk">2 of 5.</b> <span class="pdd-s3-pt">The same supplier at $20,000. ACH still costs $0.26 to $0.50, since its fee does not grow with size. Percentage prices belong to cross-border rails, so they step back.</span></p>
<div class="pdd-s3-strip" role="group" aria-label="Published prices on one log scale">

<div class="pdd-s3-chh"><p class="pdd-cap pdd-s3-lh">One $20,000 payment, log scale</p><div class="pdd-s3-sizew"><span class="pdd-s3-szl">Size</span><div class="pdd-seg pdd-s3-size" role="group" aria-label="Payment size"><button type="button" data-a="0" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="One $1,000 payment">$1K</button><button type="button" data-a="1" aria-pressed="true" aria-label="One $20,000 payment">$20K</button><button type="button" data-a="2" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="One $5 million payment">$5M</button><button type="button" data-a="3" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="One $25 million payment">$25M</button></div></div></div>

<div class="pdd-s3-rows">
<div class="pdd-s3-lr" data-m="bank" data-h="none" data-lid="fednow" data-scope="dom"><p class="pdd-s3-ln"><b>FedNow</b> customer price not published<span class="pdd-s3-lx" hidden>, over its <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="F#17" data-note="FedNow allows up to $10 million per payment.">$10 million</span> limit</span><span class="pdd-s3-nf">Network fee to bank <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="F#19" data-note="2026 fee the Fed charges the sending bank. The price a bank charges its customer is not in the sources.">$0.045</span></span></p><span class="pdd-s3-lt" aria-hidden="true"></span></div>

<details class="pdd-more pdd-s3-dw" id="pdd-s3-dw"><summary>Inputs and 8 gates</summary><div class="pdd-s3-dwb">
<div class="pdd-s3-form">
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Route</span><select data-key="route"><option value="domestic" selected>Domestic US</option><option value="cross">Cross-border</option></select></label>
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Payee country</span><select data-key="country"><option value="us" selected>United States</option><option value="br">Brazil</option><option value="other">Other</option></select></label>
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Payee status</span><select data-key="status"><option value="established" selected>Established</option><option value="new">New</option><option value="changed">Changed</option></select></label>
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Payee can receive</span><select data-key="cap"><option value="instant" selected>Instant bank</option><option value="ach">ACH-only bank</option><option value="hosted">Hosted wallet</option><option value="self">Self-hosted wallet</option></select></label>
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Stablecoin consent</span><select data-key="consent"><option value="yes">Yes</option><option value="no" selected>No</option></select></label>
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Token asked for</span><select data-key="token"><option value="usdc" selected>USDC</option><option value="usdt">USDT</option></select></label>
<div class="pdd-s3-f pdd-s3-fw" data-m="flow"><label for="pdd-s3-amt">Amount</label><input type="range" id="pdd-s3-amt" min="0" max="3" step="1" value="1" aria-valuetext="$3,000 to $1 million"><output class="pdd-cap pdd-s3-amt-out" for="pdd-s3-amt">$3,000 to $1 million</output></div>
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Travel Rule data</span><select data-key="data"><option value="yes" selected>Complete</option><option value="no">Incomplete</option></select></label>
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Speed</span><select data-key="speed"><option value="minutes">Minutes</option><option value="same">Same day</option><option value="next" selected>Next day</option></select></label>
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Day</span><select data-key="day"><option value="hours" selected>Business hours</option><option value="outside">Weekend or holiday</option></select></label>
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Partner and chain</span><select data-key="health"><option value="normal" selected>Normal</option><option value="chain">Chain or ramp down</option><option value="bank">Bank partner down</option></select></label>
<p class="pdd-s3-shut pdd-s3-fw">Shut at a weekend or holiday: <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#8 G#6" data-note="Fedwire runs Monday to Friday. Sundays and weekday holidays come no earlier than 2028.">Fedwire</span>,</span> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="G#25 D#27" data-note="Circle says redemption payouts to a bank typically settle on the next business day. USDC traded as low as 86 cents over a weekend in March 2023 while redemption was paused.">USDC redemption to a bank</span></p>
</div>
<div class="pdd-s3-stack">
<p class="pdd-label pdd-s3-gl">Eight gates, all run</p>
<div class="pdd-s3-gates" role="group" aria-label="Hard gates, in order">
<button type="button" class="pdd-s3-g" data-g="G1" data-s="info" data-m="rule" aria-pressed="true" aria-controls="pdd-s3-note"><b>G1</b><span class="pdd-s3-gn">Sanctions screening</span><span class="pdd-s3-gs">Runs</span></button>
<button type="button" class="pdd-s3-g" data-g="G2" data-s="pass" data-m="ok" aria-pressed="false" aria-controls="pdd-s3-note"><b>G2</b><span class="pdd-s3-gn">Token legality</span><span class="pdd-s3-gs">Pass</span></button>
<button type="button" class="pdd-s3-g" data-g="G3" data-s="pass" data-m="ok" aria-pressed="false" aria-controls="pdd-s3-note"><b>G3</b><span class="pdd-s3-gn">Consent and capability</span><span class="pdd-s3-gs">Pass</span></button>
<button type="button" class="pdd-s3-g" data-g="G4" data-s="pass" data-m="ok" aria-pressed="false" aria-controls="pdd-s3-note"><b>G4</b><span class="pdd-s3-gn">Amount caps</span><span class="pdd-s3-gs">Pass</span></button>
<button type="button" class="pdd-s3-g" data-g="G5" data-s="pass" data-m="ok" aria-pressed="false" aria-controls="pdd-s3-note"><b>G5</b><span class="pdd-s3-gn">Travel Rule data</span><span class="pdd-s3-gs">Pass</span></button>
<button type="button" class="pdd-s3-g" data-g="G6" data-s="pass" data-m="ok" aria-pressed="false" aria-controls="pdd-s3-note"><b>G6</b><span class="pdd-s3-gn">First payout</span><span class="pdd-s3-gs">Pass</span></button>
<button type="button" class="pdd-s3-g" data-g="G7" data-s="pass" data-m="ok" aria-pressed="false" aria-controls="pdd-s3-note"><b>G7</b><span class="pdd-s3-gn">Token allow-list</span><span class="pdd-s3-gs">Pass</span></button>
<button type="button" class="pdd-s3-g" data-g="G8" data-s="pass" data-m="ok" aria-pressed="false" aria-controls="pdd-s3-note"><b>G8</b><span class="pdd-s3-gn">Partner and chain health</span><span class="pdd-s3-gs">Pass</span></button>
</div>
<p class="pdd-s3-note" id="pdd-s3-note" data-m="rule"><b class="pdd-s3-nn">G1 Sanctions screening</b> <span class="pdd-chip pdd-s3-gw" data-src="G#16 G#17 F#8">Parties and addresses, again at submission</span></p>
<p class="pdd-label pdd-s3-gl">Why not the others</p>
<ul class="pdd-s3-not"><li>Same Day ACH: speed not needed</li><li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="F#16" data-note="The Clearing House network page, undated.">RTP or FedNow: irrevocable, no need</span></li><li>Stablecoin: never chosen for domestic</li></ul>
</div>
</div></details>
<p class="pdd-s3-sr" aria-live="polite"></p>
</div>
<p class="pdd-say"><span class="pdd-view">Mapping inputs to a rail is our view.</span> Stripe lists <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="F#27" data-note="Stripe stablecoin payouts are in private preview with no published price. Its 1.5% list price is for accepting stablecoin payments, which is a different service.">1.5%</span> to accept stablecoin payments and no price for payouts.</p>
</section>
<section class="pdd-scene pdd-s5-scene" id="pdd-rules" aria-label="Rules and open questions">
<p class="pdd-kicker"><b>6</b>Rules and open questions</p>
<h2 class="pdd-q">What do the rules say, and what stays open?</h2>
<p class="pdd-a">The OCC issued its final issuer rule on <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2 C#12" data-note="OCC final rule for stablecoin issuers, effective January 18, 2027. AML and sanctions parts come in a separate Treasury rulemaking.">October 21, 2026</span>. The other four agencies are still at proposal, and no rule blocks paying a supplier in an <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="C#10" data-note="The rules fall mainly on issuers.">existing stablecoin</span>.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: which rules change your payouts, and when.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: the rules to build in before launch.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">For you: the dates that move the market.</span></p>
<div class="pdd-stage">
<p class="pdd-s5-sr">Summary: the OCC issued its final GENIUS issuer rule on October 21, 2026, effective January 18, 2027. The Federal Reserve, Treasury, the FDIC and the NCUA each have a GENIUS proposal that is not final. Treasury shows both the August 18, 2026 proposed rule and the September 30, 2026 interim final rule, which is procedural only. The Act takes effect on January 18, 2027 and the ban on non-permitted stablecoins starts on July 18, 2028.</p>
<div class="pdd-grid">
<ul class="pdd-s5-ags" aria-label="Five US agencies, GENIUS rule status">
<li class="pdd-s5-ag pdd-panel" data-m="rule" data-rise><div class="pdd-s5-agtop"><svg class="pdd-s5-glyph" viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><circle class="pdd-s5-ring" cx="40" cy="40" r="35"/><path d="M29 21h15l9 9v29H29zM44 21v9h9M35 41h12M35 49h12"/><circle class="pdd-s5-badge" cx="62" cy="62" r="13"/><path class="pdd-s5-tick" d="M56 62l4 4 8-9"/></svg><b class="pdd-s5-name">OCC</b></div><p class="pdd-s5-line"><span>Proposed</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="C#11">February 25, 2026</span></p><p class="pdd-s5-line" data-m="ok"><span>Final rule</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2" data-note="Issued October 21, 2026, effective January 18, 2027. AML and sanctions parts come in a separate Treasury rulemaking.">October 21, 2026</span></p></li>
<li class="pdd-s5-ag pdd-panel" data-m="rule" data-rise><div class="pdd-s5-agtop"><svg class="pdd-s5-glyph" viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><circle class="pdd-s5-ring" cx="40" cy="40" r="35"/><path d="M29 21h15l9 9v29H29zM44 21v9h9M35 41h12M35 49h12"/></svg><b class="pdd-s5-name">Federal Reserve</b></div><p class="pdd-s5-line"><span>Proposed</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="C#13" data-note="Two proposals.">September 24, 2026</span></p><p class="pdd-s5-line"><span>Comments close</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="C#14">November 30, 2026</span></p></li>
<li class="pdd-s5-ag pdd-panel" data-m="rule" data-rise><div class="pdd-s5-agtop"><svg class="pdd-s5-glyph" viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><circle class="pdd-s5-ring" cx="40" cy="40" r="35"/><path d="M29 21h15l9 9v29H29zM44 21v9h9M35 41h12M35 49h12"/><circle class="pdd-s5-badge" cx="62" cy="62" r="13"/><path class="pdd-s5-tick" d="M56 62l4 4 8-9"/></svg><b class="pdd-s5-name">Treasury</b></div><p class="pdd-s5-line"><span>Proposed rule</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="F#1" data-note="Section 3. Comments close October 19, 2026.">August 18, 2026</span></p><p class="pdd-s5-line" data-m="ok"><span>Interim final rule, procedural only</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="F#2 G#2" data-note="Effective, with certifications not yet accepted.">September 30, 2026</span></p></li>
<li class="pdd-s5-ag pdd-panel" data-m="rule" data-rise><div class="pdd-s5-agtop"><svg class="pdd-s5-glyph" viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><circle class="pdd-s5-ring" cx="40" cy="40" r="35"/><path d="M29 21h15l9 9v29H29zM44 21v9h9M35 41h12M35 49h12"/></svg><b class="pdd-s5-name">FDIC</b></div><p class="pdd-s5-line"><span>Proposed</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="C#16">April 7, 2026</span></p><p class="pdd-s5-line"><span>In the Federal Register</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="C#16">April 10, 2026</span></p></li>
<li class="pdd-s5-ag pdd-panel" data-m="rule" data-rise><div class="pdd-s5-agtop"><svg class="pdd-s5-glyph" viewBox="0 0 80 80" aria-hidden="true"><circle class="pdd-s5-ring" cx="40" cy="40" r="35"/><path d="M29 21h15l9 9v29H29zM44 21v9h9M35 41h12M35 49h12"/></svg><b class="pdd-s5-name">NCUA</b></div><p class="pdd-s5-line"><span>Proposed</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="C#17">May 15, 2026</span></p><p class="pdd-s5-line"><span>Comments closed</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="C#17" data-note="No final NCUA rule found.">July 17, 2026</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div class="pdd-s5-tl pdd-panel pdd-c8">
<div class="pdd-s5-tlh"><p class="pdd-cap">Research, not advice. </div>
<div class="pdd-s5-qs pdd-c4">
<p class="pdd-label">Three open questions for your seat</p>
<ol class="pdd-s5-ql" data-for="buyer"><li class="pdd-s5-qc"><b class="pdd-s5-qn" aria-hidden="true">1</b><p class="pdd-cap">What is the firm <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#21" data-note="One central bank test found no systematic advantage.">all-in quote</span> for this corridor?</p><a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-chooser">Screen 5: which rail fits</a></li><li class="pdd-s5-qc"><b class="pdd-s5-qn" aria-hidden="true">2</b><p class="pdd-cap">Who bears <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="G#31" data-note="One failure left users short. The cause was not known.">each loss</span>?</p><a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-path">Screen 4: the money path</a></li><li class="pdd-s5-qc"><b class="pdd-s5-qn" aria-hidden="true">3</b><p class="pdd-cap">Which AI result has <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="H#13" data-note="One vendor figure rests on a single bill.">evidence</span> beyond the vendor?</p><a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-ai">Screen 2: where AI fits</a></li></ol>
<ol class="pdd-s5-ql" data-for="builder"><li class="pdd-s5-qc"><b class="pdd-s5-qn" aria-hidden="true">1</b><p class="pdd-cap">Can one instruction ever <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#29" data-note="Stripe says keys can be pruned after 24 hours.">send twice</span>?</p><a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-path">Screen 4: the money path</a></li><li class="pdd-s5-qc"><b class="pdd-s5-qn" aria-hidden="true">2</b><p class="pdd-cap">Which gate does an agent never <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="H#30" data-note="No live bank deployment found releases a payment unsupervised.">pass alone</span>?</p><a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-ai">Screen 2: where AI fits</a></li><li class="pdd-s5-qc"><b class="pdd-s5-qn" aria-hidden="true">3</b><p class="pdd-cap">Does a rail change reopen the <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="G#20" data-note="The rule is not limited to one rail.">Travel Rule</span> check?</p><a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-chooser">Screen 5: which rail fits</a></li></ol>
<ol class="pdd-s5-ql" data-for="strategy"><li class="pdd-s5-qc"><b class="pdd-s5-qn" aria-hidden="true">1</b><p class="pdd-cap">Who prices AI <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#21" data-note="BILL lists no separate price for its agents.">per outcome</span> first?</p><a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-ai">Screen 2: where AI fits</a></li><li class="pdd-s5-qc"><b class="pdd-s5-qn" aria-hidden="true">2</b><p class="pdd-cap">Do falling <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#5">take rates</span> outrun volume growth?</p><a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-answer">Screen 1: the answer</a></li><li class="pdd-s5-qc"><b class="pdd-s5-qn" aria-hidden="true">3</b><p class="pdd-cap">Does the FCA change rules for <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="I#31" data-note="Wording from a law firm summary.">agent payments</span>?</p><a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-ai">Screen 2: where AI fits</a></li></ol>
